```
for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const product = products.nth(i);
  const name = await product.locator('.product-name').textContent();
  const price = await product.locator('.product-price').textContent();
  console.log(`${name}: ${price}`);
}
```

Using .all() (Better):
```
const products = await page.locator('.product').all();

for (const product of products) {
  const name = await product.locator('.product-name').textContent();
  const price = await product.locator('.product-price').textContent();
  console.log(`${name}: ${price}`);
}
```

Filter and Process:
```
const expensiveProducts = await page.locator('.product')
  .filter({ has: page.locator('.price:has-text("$100+")') })
  .all();

console.log(`Found ${expensiveProducts.length} expensive products`);
```

Advanced Locator Techniques:
-----------------------------

1. Has-text Pseudo-class:
```
page.locator('button:has-text("Submit")')
page.locator('article:has-text("Breaking News")')
```

2. Visible Elements Only:
```
page.locator('button >> visible=true')
```

3. N-th Match:
```
page.locator('button >> nth=0')  // First
page.locator('button >> nth=-1') // Last
```

4. Multiple Selectors (OR):
```
page.locator('button, a.button, input[type="submit"]')
```

5. Chaining:
```
page.locator('.container')
    .locator('article')
    .filter({ hasText: 'Featured' })
    .locator('button')
    .first()
```

Frame Locators:
---------------

Working with iframes:
```
// Locate frame
const frame = page.frameLocator('iframe#myframe');

// Elements inside frame
await frame.locator('#username').fill('user');
await frame.locator('#password').fill('pass');
await frame.getByRole('button', { name: 'Login' }).click();
```

Shadow DOM:
-----------

Playwright handles Shadow DOM automatically:
```
// Works even if #button is inside shadow DOM
await page.locator('#button').click();
```

No special syntax needed!

Debugging Locators:
-------------------

1. Codegen (Best Method):
```
npx playwright codegen https://example.com
```
Click elements to generate selectors

2. Playwright Inspector:
```
await page.pause();
```
Interactive debugger with locator playground

3. Console Logging:
```
const locator = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' });
console.log(await locator.count());
console.log(await locator.textContent());
```

4. Screenshot:
```
await page.locator('.problem-area').screenshot({ path: 'debug.png' });
```

Locator Performance:
--------------------

Fast:
✓ page.getByTestId('id')
✓ page.getByRole('button')
✓ page.locator('#id')

Slower:
⚠️ Complex CSS selectors
⚠️ XPath
⚠️ Text searches on large pages

Optimization:
```
// ❌ Slow (searches entire page each time)
for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
  await page.locator('.item').nth(i).click();
}

// ✓ Fast (gets all once)
const items = await page.locator('.item').all();
for (const item of items) {
  await item.click();
}
```

================================================================================

11. AUTO-WAITING MECHANISM
===========================

The Revolutionary Feature:
--------------------------

Auto-waiting is Playwright's killer feature that eliminates the #1 cause of
flaky tests: race conditions.

Traditional Automation (Selenium):
```
driver.get("https://example.com");
// Hope page loaded...
WebElement button = driver.findElement(By.id("submit"));
// Hope element exists...
button.click();
// Hope it's clickable...
```

Result: Intermittent "Element not found" or "Element not clickable" errors

Playwright:
```
await page.goto('https://example.com');
await page.click('#submit');
```

Result: Reliable, consistent execution!

What Auto-Waiting Does:
------------------------

Before EVERY action, Playwright automatically waits for:

1. Element to be attached to DOM
2. Element to be visible
3. Element to be stable (not animating)
4. Element to receive events (not obscured)
5. Element to be enabled (if applicable)

All this happens automatically - no explicit waits needed!

Actions That Auto-Wait:
-----------------------

Complete List:

1. click():
   - Attached to DOM
   - Visible
   - Stable
   - Receives events
   - Enabled

2. fill():
   - Attached
   - Visible
   - Enabled
   - Editable

3. check() / uncheck():
   - Attached
   - Visible
   - Stable
   - Enabled

4. selectOption():
   - Attached
   - Visible
   - Enabled

5. press():
   - Attached
   - Stable

6. hover():
   - Attached
   - Visible
   - Stable
   - Receives events

7. type():
   - Attached
   - Visible
   - Enabled

Auto-Waiting in Action:
-----------------------

Example 1: Dynamic Button
```html
<!-- Button appears after 2 seconds -->
<script>
  setTimeout(() => {
    document.body.innerHTML += '<button id="submit">Submit</button>';
  }, 2000);
</script>
```

Playwright Test:
```
test('click dynamic button', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/page');
  
  // No explicit wait needed!
  // Playwright automatically waits up to 30 seconds for button
  await page.click('#submit');
  
  // ✓ Test passes reliably
});
```

Selenium Equivalent (Requires explicit wait):
```
WebDriverWait wait = new WebDriverWait(driver, 30);
WebElement button = wait.until(
  ExpectedConditions.elementToBeClickable(By.id("submit"))
);
button.click();
```

Example 2: Loading Spinner
```html
<!-- Spinner shown during load -->
<div id="loading-spinner">Loading...</div>
<button id="submit" style="display:none">Submit</button>

<script>
  setTimeout(() => {
    document.getElementById('loading-spinner').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('submit').style.display = 'block';
  }, 3000);
</script>
```

Playwright Test:
```
test('wait for loading', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/page');
  
  // Automatically waits for spinner to disappear
  // and button to become visible!
  await page.click('#submit');
});
```

Example 3: Animation
```html
<!-- Button slides in -->
<button id="menu" style="transform: translateX(-100px); transition: 1s">
  Menu
</button>

<script>
  setTimeout(() => {
    document.getElementById('menu').style.transform = 'translateX(0)';
  }, 100);
</script>
```

Playwright Test:
```
test('click animated button', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/page');
  
  // Waits for animation to complete!
  // Element must be stable for 100ms
  await page.click('#menu');
});
```

Actionability Checks:
---------------------

Playwright performs these checks before actions:

1. Attached:
   ```
   Element must be present in DOM
   ✓ <button id="btn">Click</button>
   ✗ Element removed from DOM
   ```

2. Visible:
   ```
   Element must be visible
   ✓ display: block, visibility: visible, opacity > 0
   ✗ display: none, visibility: hidden, opacity: 0
   ```

3. Stable:
   ```
   Element position must not change
   - Waits for animations to complete
   - Two consecutive checks 100ms apart
   - Position and size must match
   ```

4. Receives Events:
   ```
   Element must be able to receive pointer events
   ✓ Not covered by another element
   ✗ Covered by modal, overlay, etc.
   ```

5. Enabled:
   ```
   Element must be enabled (for inputs)
   ✓ <input>
   ✗ <input disabled>
   ```

Real-World Scenarios:
---------------------

Scenario 1: AJAX Form Submission
```
test('submit form', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/form');
  
  await page.fill('#name', 'John Doe');
  await page.fill('#email', 'john@example.com');
  
  // Click submit - form submits via AJAX
  await page.click('#submit');
  
  // Success message appears after server response
  // Auto-waits for the message to appear!
  await expect(page.locator('.success')).toHaveText('Form submitted!');
});
```

Scenario 2: Modal Dialog
```
test('close modal', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/page');
  
  // Open modal
  await page.click('#open-modal');
  
  // Modal fades in with CSS animation
  // Auto-waits for close button to be clickable!
  await page.click('#close-modal');
  
  // Verify modal closed
  await expect(page.locator('.modal')).toBeHidden();
});
```

Scenario 3: Infinite Scroll
```
test('load more items', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/infinite-scroll');
  
  // Initial items
  await expect(page.locator('.item')).toHaveCount(20);
  
  // Scroll to bottom - triggers load
  await page.locator('.item').last().scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  
  // New items appear asynchronously
  // Auto-waits for 40 items to appear!
  await expect(page.locator('.item')).toHaveCount(40);
});
```

Timeout Configuration:
----------------------

Default Timeouts:

```
// Default action timeout: 30 seconds
await page.click('#button');  // Waits up to 30s

// Default navigation timeout: 30 seconds
await page.goto('https://example.com');  // Waits up to 30s

// Default assertion timeout: 5 seconds
await expect(locator).toBeVisible();  // Waits up to 5s
```

Custom Timeouts:

1. Global Configuration (playwright.config.ts):
```
export default defineConfig({
  timeout: 60000,  // Test timeout: 60s
  
  expect: {
    timeout: 10000  // Assertion timeout: 10s
  },
  
  use: {
    actionTimeout: 15000,       // Action timeout: 15s
    navigationTimeout: 30000,   // Navigation timeout: 30s
  },
});
```

2. Per Action:
```
await page.click('#button', { timeout: 10000 });  // 10s timeout
await page.fill('#input', 'text', { timeout: 5000 });  // 5s timeout
```

3. Per Assertion:
```
await expect(locator).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });  // 10s timeout
```

4. Per Test:
```
test('slow test', async ({ page }) => {
  test.setTimeout(120000);  // 2 minutes for this test
  
  // Long-running operations...
});
```

When Auto-Waiting Isn't Enough:
--------------------------------

Sometimes you need explicit waits:

1. Wait for Specific State:
```
// Wait for element to be hidden
await page.waitForSelector('#loading', { state: 'hidden' });

// Wait for element to be attached
await page.waitForSelector('#button', { state: 'attached' });

// Wait for element to be detached
await page.waitForSelector('#modal', { state: 'detached' });
```

2. Wait for Network:
```
// Wait for specific response
await page.waitForResponse('**/api/users');

// Wait for specific request
await page.waitForRequest('**/api/save');

// Wait for load state
await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle');
```

3. Wait for Function:
```
// Wait for custom condition
await page.waitForFunction(() => {
  return document.querySelectorAll('.item').length > 10;
});

// With polling
await page.waitForFunction(() => {
  return window.dataLoaded === true;
}, { polling: 100 });  // Check every 100ms
```

4. Wait for URL:
```
await page.waitForURL('**/dashboard');
await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard/);
```

5. Wait for Timeout (Discouraged):
```
// ❌ Avoid - makes tests slower and flakier
await page.waitForTimeout(2000);  // Hard-coded 2s wait

// ✓ Better - wait for specific condition
await page.waitForSelector('#content');
```

Load States:
------------

Playwright tracks page load states:

1. load:
   - DOMContentLoaded event fired
   - Page is interactive

2. domcontentloaded:
   - DOM fully loaded and parsed
   - Earliest load state

3. networkidle:
   - No network activity for 500ms
   - All AJAX requests finished

Usage:
```
await page.goto('https://example.com', { 
  waitUntil: 'networkidle' 
});

// Or wait separately
await page.goto('https://example.com');
await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle');
```

Common Patterns:
----------------

Pattern 1: Wait for Navigation
```
// Click triggers navigation
await Promise.all([
  page.waitForNavigation(),
  page.click('#link')
]);
```

Pattern 2: Wait for Response
```
const [response] = await Promise.all([
  page.waitForResponse('**/api/data'),
  page.click('#load-data')
]);

const data = await response.json();
console.log(data);
```

Pattern 3: Wait for Multiple Conditions
```
await Promise.all([
  page.waitForSelector('#content'),
  page.waitForLoadState('networkidle'),
  page.waitForFunction(() => window.appReady === true)
]);
```

Assertions with Auto-Retry:
----------------------------

Playwright assertions auto-retry until timeout:

```
// Retries for 5 seconds (default)
await expect(page.locator('.message')).toHaveText('Success');

// Behind the scenes:
// Check 1: Text is "Loading..." → retry
// Check 2: Text is "Loading..." → retry
// Check 3: Text is "Success" → pass!
```

Soft vs Hard Assertions:
```
// Hard assertion (stops test on failure)
await expect(locator).toHaveText('Expected');

// Soft assertion (continues test on failure)
await expect.soft(locator).toHaveText('Expected');
```

Debugging Auto-Waiting:
-----------------------

1. Verbose Logging:
```
DEBUG=pw:api npx playwright test
```

2. Trace Viewer:
```
npx playwright test --trace on
npx playwright show-trace trace.zip
```
Shows exactly when Playwright waited and why

3. Screenshots:
```
await page.screenshot({ path: 'before-click.png' });
await page.click('#button');
await page.screenshot({ path: 'after-click.png' });
```

4. Slow Motion:
```
// In config or test
use: {
  launchOptions: {
    slowMo: 1000  // 1 second delay between actions
  }
}
```

Best Practices:
---------------

1. ✅ Trust Auto-Waiting:
   ```
   // ✓ Simple and reliable
   await page.click('#button');
   
   // ✗ Unnecessary
   await page.waitForSelector('#button');
   await page.click('#button');
   ```

2. ✅ Use Specific Waits When Needed:
   ```
   // ✓ Wait for specific network request
   await page.waitForResponse('**/api/save');
   
   // ✗ Arbitrary timeout
   await page.waitForTimeout(2000);
   ```

3. ✅ Configure Appropriate Timeouts:
   ```
   // ✓ Reasonable global timeout
   timeout: 30000
   
   // ✗ Too short (causes failures)
   timeout: 1000
   
   // ✗ Too long (slow feedback)
   timeout: 300000
   ```

4. ✅ Use Assertions for Verification:
   ```
   // ✓ Auto-retries
   await expect(locator).toBeVisible();
   
   // ✗ One-time check (might fail randomly)
   const visible = await locator.isVisible();
   expect(visible).toBe(true);
   ```

================================================================================

This completes **Part II: Core Features and Practices** sections 9-11. 

I'll continue with sections 12-15 to finish Part II in the next update.

12. ASSERTIONS AND VALIDATIONS
===============================

Playwright's Assertion Library:
--------------------------------

Playwright uses expect from @playwright/test with auto-retrying capabilities.

Key Difference from Jest/Jasmine:
- Standard expect: One-time check
- Playwright expect: Retries until timeout

Example:
```
// Standard expect (Jest)
expect(value).toBe(5);  // Checks once, fails if not 5

// Playwright expect
await expect(locator).toHaveText('Success');  
// Retries for 5 seconds, passes when text becomes 'Success'
```

Web Assertions:
---------------

1. Visibility Assertions:
```
await expect(locator).toBeVisible()
await expect(locator).toBeHidden()
await expect(locator).not.toBeVisible()
```

Real-world example:
```
test('modal appears', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.click('#open-modal');
  
  // Modal fades in over 300ms
  // Auto-retries until visible
  await expect(page.locator('.modal')).toBeVisible();
});
```

2. Text Assertions:
```
await expect(locator).toHaveText('exact text')
await expect(locator).toHaveText(/regex pattern/)
await expect(locator).toHaveText(['array', 'of', 'texts'])
await expect(locator).toContainText('partial text')
```

Options:
```
await expect(locator).toHaveText('text', { 
  ignoreCase: true,
  useInnerText: true 
})
```

Example:
```
test('welcome message', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/dashboard');
  
  // Exact match
  await expect(page.locator('.welcome')).toHaveText('Welcome, John!');
  
  // Partial match
  await expect(page.locator('.welcome')).toContainText('Welcome');
  
  // Case-insensitive
  await expect(page.locator('.welcome')).toHaveText('welcome, john!', {
    ignoreCase: true
  });
});
```

3. Input Value Assertions:
```
await expect(locator).toHaveValue('value')
await expect(locator).toHaveValue(/pattern/)
```

Example:
```
test('form prefilled', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/edit-profile');
  
  await expect(page.locator('#email')).toHaveValue('john@example.com');
  await expect(page.locator('#age')).toHaveValue('25');
});
```

4. Attribute Assertions:
```
await expect(locator).toHaveAttribute('name', 'value')
await expect(locator).toHaveAttribute('href', /pattern/)
await expect(locator).toHaveAttribute('disabled', '')
```

Example:
```
test('link attributes', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  
  const link = page.locator('a.external');
  await expect(link).toHaveAttribute('href', 'https://example.com');
  await expect(link).toHaveAttribute('target', '_blank');
  await expect(link).toHaveAttribute('rel', 'noopener');
});
```

5. CSS Assertions:
```
await expect(locator).toHaveCSS('property', 'value')
```

Example:
```
test('button styles', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  
  const button = page.locator('.primary-btn');
  await expect(button).toHaveCSS('background-color', 'rgb(0, 123, 255)');
  await expect(button).toHaveCSS('color', 'rgb(255, 255, 255)');
});
```

6. Class Assertions:
```
await expect(locator).toHaveClass('classname')
await expect(locator).toHaveClass(/pattern/)
await expect(locator).toHaveClass(['class1', 'class2'])
```

Example:
```
test('active tab', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.click('#tab-2');
  
  await expect(page.locator('#tab-2')).toHaveClass(/active/);
  await expect(page.locator('#tab-1')).not.toHaveClass(/active/);
});
```

7. Count Assertions:
```
await expect(locator).toHaveCount(number)
```

Example:
```
test('todo list', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/todos');
  
  // Initially 3 todos
  await expect(page.locator('.todo')).toHaveCount(3);
  
  // Add new todo
  await page.fill('.new-todo', 'New task');
  await page.press('.new-todo', 'Enter');
  
  // Now 4 todos
  await expect(page.locator('.todo')).toHaveCount(4);
});
```

8. State Assertions:
```
await expect(locator).toBeEnabled()
await expect(locator).toBeDisabled()
await expect(locator).toBeEditable()
await expect(locator).toBeChecked()
await expect(locator).toBeEmpty()
await expect(locator).toBeFocused()
```

Example:
```
test('form validation', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/form');
  
  const submit = page.locator('#submit');
  
  // Initially disabled
  await expect(submit).toBeDisabled();
  
  // Fill required fields
  await page.fill('#email', 'test@example.com');
  await page.fill('#password', 'password123');
  
  // Now enabled
  await expect(submit).toBeEnabled();
  
  // Checkbox checked
  await page.check('#terms');
  await expect(page.locator('#terms')).toBeChecked();
});
```

Page Assertions:
----------------

1. URL Assertions:
```
await expect(page).toHaveURL('https://example.com/page')
await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/page$/)
await expect(page).toHaveURL(/page/)
```

Example:
```
test('navigation', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/login');
  
  await page.fill('#username', 'user');
  await page.fill('#password', 'pass');
  await page.click('#login');
  
  // Verify redirected
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/dashboard');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/dashboard/);
});
```

2. Title Assertions:
```
await expect(page).toHaveTitle('Page Title')
await expect(page).toHaveTitle(/Title/)
```

Example:
```
test('page title', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(page).toHaveTitle('Home - My Website');
  
  await page.click('a[href="/about"]');
  await expect(page).toHaveTitle(/About/);
});
```

3. Screenshot Assertions:
```
await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('screenshot.png')
await expect(locator).toHaveScreenshot('element.png')
```

Example:
```
test('visual regression', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  
  // First run: generates baseline
  // Subsequent runs: compares against baseline
  await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('homepage.png');
  
  // Element-specific
  await expect(page.locator('.hero')).toHaveScreenshot('hero-section.png');
});
```

API Response Assertions:
------------------------

```
test('API test', async ({ request }) => {
  const response = await request.get('https://api.example.com/users');
  
  // Status
  expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy();
  expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
  
  // Headers
  expect(response.headers()['content-type']).toContain('application/json');
  
  // Body
  const data = await response.json();
  expect(data.users).toHaveLength(10);
  expect(data.users[0]).toHaveProperty('name');
});
```

Negation:
---------

Use .not for opposite assertion:
```
await expect(locator).not.toBeVisible()
await expect(locator).not.toHaveText('wrong')
await expect(locator).not.toHaveClass('inactive')
await expect(page).not.toHaveURL('/old-page')
```

Soft Assertions:
----------------

Continue test even if assertion fails:

```
test('multiple checks', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  
  // Soft assertions - test continues even if these fail
  await expect.soft(page.locator('.header')).toBeVisible();
  await expect.soft(page.locator('.footer')).toBeVisible();
  await expect.soft(page.locator('.sidebar')).toBeVisible();
  
  // Test continues here regardless of failures above
  await page.click('#button');
  
  // Regular assertion - stops test if fails
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/next-page');
});
```

Use case: Check multiple elements without stopping at first failure

Custom Assertions:
------------------

Extend expect with custom matchers:

```
import { expect as baseExpect } from '@playwright/test';

export const expect = baseExpect.extend({
  async toHaveValidEmail(locator: Locator) {
    const value = await locator.inputValue();
    const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
    
    const pass = emailRegex.test(value);
    
    return {
      pass,
      message: () => pass 
        ? `Expected ${value} not to be valid email`
        : `Expected ${value} to be valid email`
    };
  }
});

// Usage:
await expect(page.locator('#email')).toHaveValidEmail();
```

Polling Assertions:
-------------------

Wait for condition with custom polling:

```
test('wait for data load', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  
  // Wait for function to return true
  await expect(async () => {
    const count = await page.locator('.item').count();
    expect(count).toBeGreaterThan(10);
  }).toPass();
  
  // Options
  await expect(async () => {
    const text = await page.locator('.status').textContent();
    expect(text).toBe('Complete');
  }).toPass({
    timeout: 30000,      // 30 seconds
    intervals: [1000]    // Check every 1 second
  });
});
```

Assertion Timeout:
------------------

Configure timeout per assertion:

```
// Default: 5 seconds
await expect(locator).toBeVisible();

// Custom timeout: 10 seconds
await expect(locator).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });

// Global config (playwright.config.ts)
export default defineConfig({
  expect: {
    timeout: 10000  // All assertions wait 10s
  }
});
```

Combining Assertions:
---------------------

Multiple assertions on same element:

```
test('button state', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  
  const button = page.locator('#submit');
  
  // Multiple assertions
  await expect(button).toBeVisible();
  await expect(button).toBeEnabled();
  await expect(button).toHaveText('Submit');
  await expect(button).toHaveClass('btn-primary');
  await expect(button).toHaveAttribute('type', 'submit');
});
```

Real-World Assertion Patterns:
-------------------------------

Pattern 1: Form Submission
```
test('submit form', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/contact');
  
  // Fill form
  await page.fill('#name', 'John Doe');
  await page.fill('#email', 'john@example.com');
  await page.fill('#message', 'Hello!');
  
  // Submit
  await page.click('#submit');
  
  // Verify success
  await expect(page.locator('.success-message')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator('.success-message')).toHaveText(
    'Thank you for your message!'
  );
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/contact?submitted=true');
  
  // Form cleared
  await expect(page.locator('#name')).toHaveValue('');
  await expect(page.locator('#email')).toHaveValue('');
});
```

Pattern 2: Search Results
```
test('search products', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  
  await page.fill('#search', 'laptop');
  await page.press('#search', 'Enter');
  
  // Wait for results
  await expect(page.locator('.search-results')).toBeVisible();
  
  // Check count
  const results = page.locator('.product-card');
  await expect(results).toHaveCount(12);
  
  // Check first result
  await expect(results.first()).toContainText('laptop');
  
  // Check all results contain search term
  const count = await results.count();
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    await expect(results.nth(i)).toContainText('laptop', {
      ignoreCase: true
    });
  }
});
```

Pattern 3: Dynamic Content
```
test('live updates', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/dashboard');
  
  // Initial state
  await expect(page.locator('.notification-count')).toHaveText('0');
  
  // Trigger update (simulated server push)
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    setTimeout(() => {
      document.querySelector('.notification-count').textContent = '3';
    }, 2000);
  });
  
  // Auto-retries for 5 seconds
  await expect(page.locator('.notification-count')).toHaveText('3');
});
```

Common Mistakes:
----------------

1. ❌ Using Non-Async Checks:
```
// Wrong - one-time check, no retry
const visible = await locator.isVisible();
expect(visible).toBe(true);

// Right - auto-retries
await expect(locator).toBeVisible();
```

2. ❌ Awaiting Sync expect:
```
// Wrong - regular expect doesn't need await
await expect(5).toBe(5);

// Right
expect(5).toBe(5);

// But Playwright locator assertions DO need await
await expect(locator).toBeVisible();
```

3. ❌ Too Short Timeout:
```
// Might fail on slow CI
await expect(locator).toBeVisible({ timeout: 100 });

// Better - reasonable timeout
await expect(locator).toBeVisible({ timeout: 5000 });
```

Best Practices:
---------------

1. ✅ Use Web-First Assertions:
   - toBeVisible(), toHaveText(), etc.
   - Auto-retry built-in
   - More reliable

2. ✅ Be Specific:
   ```
   // ✓ Specific
   await expect(button).toHaveText('Submit Form');
   
   // ✗ Vague
   await expect(button).toBeVisible();
   ```

3. ✅ Test User Perspective:
   ```
   // ✓ What user sees
   await expect(page.locator('.error')).toHaveText('Invalid email');
   
   // ✗ Implementation detail
   await expect(page.locator('.error')).toHaveClass('error-red-500');
   ```

4. ✅ Use Soft Assertions for Multiple Checks:
   ```
   await expect.soft(element1).toBeVisible();
   await expect.soft(element2).toBeVisible();
   await expect.soft(element3).toBeVisible();
   // See all failures at once
   ```

================================================================================

13. PAGE OBJECT MODEL
=====================

What is Page Object Model (POM)?
---------------------------------

Page Object Model is a design pattern that:
1. Encapsulates page elements and actions
2. Reduces code duplication
3. Makes tests more maintainable
4. Separates test logic from page structure

Without POM (Anti-Pattern):
----------------------------

```
test('login test 1', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('https://example.com/login');
  await page.fill('#username', 'user1');
  await page.fill('#password', 'pass1');
  await page.click('#login-button');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/dashboard');
});

test('login test 2', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('https://example.com/login');
  await page.fill('#username', 'user2');
  await page.fill('#password', 'pass2');
  await page.click('#login-button');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/dashboard');
});
```

Problems:
- Duplicate code
- Hard to maintain (if #username changes, update everywhere)
- Mixed concerns (navigation + actions + assertions)

With POM (Best Practice):
--------------------------

LoginPage.ts:
```
import { Page, Locator } from '@playwright/test';

export class LoginPage {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly usernameInput: Locator;
  readonly passwordInput: Locator;
  readonly loginButton: Locator;
  readonly errorMessage: Locator;

  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.usernameInput = page.locator('#username');
    this.passwordInput = page.locator('#password');
    this.loginButton = page.locator('#login-button');
    this.errorMessage = page.locator('.error-message');
  }

  async goto() {
    await this.page.goto('https://example.com/login');
  }

  async login(username: string, password: string) {
    await this.usernameInput.fill(username);
    await this.passwordInput.fill(password);
    await this.loginButton.click();
  }

  async expectErrorMessage(message: string) {
    await expect(this.errorMessage).toHaveText(message);
  }
}
```

Test using POM:
```
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { LoginPage } from './pages/LoginPage';

test('login test 1', async ({ page }) => {
  const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
  await loginPage.goto();
  await loginPage.login('user1', 'pass1');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/dashboard');
});

test('login test 2', async ({ page }) => {
  const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
  await loginPage.goto();
  await loginPage.login('user2', 'pass2');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/dashboard');
});
```

Benefits:
- Single source of truth for locators
- Easy to maintain (change #username in one place)
- Readable tests
- Reusable across tests

Complete Page Object Example:
------------------------------

DashboardPage.ts:
```
import { Page, Locator, expect } from '@playwright/test';

export class DashboardPage {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly welcomeMessage: Locator;
  readonly logoutButton: Locator;
  readonly profileLink: Locator;
  readonly notificationBadge: Locator;
  readonly searchInput: Locator;

  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.welcomeMessage = page.locator('.welcome-message');
    this.logoutButton = page.locator('#logout');
    this.profileLink = page.locator('a[href="/profile"]');
    this.notificationBadge = page.locator('.notification-badge');
    this.searchInput = page.locator('#search');
  }

  async goto() {
    await this.page.goto('/dashboard');
  }

  async expectWelcomeMessage(username: string) {
    await expect(this.welcomeMessage).toHaveText(`Welcome, ${username}!`);
  }

  async logout() {
    await this.logoutButton.click();
  }

  async goToProfile() {
    await this.profileLink.click();
  }

  async search(query: string) {
    await this.searchInput.fill(query);
    await this.searchInput.press('Enter');
  }

  async getNotificationCount(): Promise<number> {
    const text = await this.notificationBadge.textContent();
    return parseInt(text || '0');
  }

  async expectNotificationCount(count: number) {
    await expect(this.notificationBadge).toHaveText(count.toString());
  }
}
```

Advanced POM Pattern - Fluent Interface:
-----------------------------------------

ProductPage.ts:
```
export class ProductPage {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly addToCartButton: Locator;
  readonly quantityInput: Locator;
  readonly sizeDropdown: Locator;

  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.addToCartButton = page.locator('#add-to-cart');
    this.quantityInput = page.locator('#quantity');
    this.sizeDropdown = page.locator('#size');
  }

  async goto(productId: string) {
    await this.page.goto(`/products/${productId}`);
    return this;  // Return this for chaining
  }

  async selectSize(size: string) {
    await this.sizeDropdown.selectOption(size);
    return this;
  }

  async setQuantity(quantity: number) {
    await this.quantityInput.fill(quantity.toString());
    return this;
  }

  async addToCart() {
    await this.addToCartButton.click();
    return this;
  }
}
```

Usage with chaining:
```
test('add product to cart', async ({ page }) => {
  const productPage = new ProductPage(page);
  
  await productPage
    .goto('123')
    .then(p => p.selectSize('L'))
    .then(p => p.setQuantity(2))
    .then(p => p.addToCart());
  
  // Or with await at each step
  await (await (await productPage.goto('123'))
    .selectSize('L'))
    .setQuantity(2);
});
```

Component Objects (Reusable Components):
-----------------------------------------

NavigationComponent.ts:
```
export class NavigationComponent {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly homeLink: Locator;
  readonly productsLink: Locator;
  readonly cartLink: Locator;
  readonly accountDropdown: Locator;

  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    const nav = page.locator('nav.main-navigation');
    this.homeLink = nav.locator('a[href="/"]');
    this.productsLink = nav.locator('a[href="/products"]');
    this.cartLink = nav.locator('a[href="/cart"]');
    this.accountDropdown = nav.locator('.account-dropdown');
  }

  async goHome() {
    await this.homeLink.click();
  }

  async goToProducts() {
    await this.productsLink.click();
  }

  async goToCart() {
    await this.cartLink.click();
  }

  async openAccountMenu() {
    await this.accountDropdown.click();
  }
}
```

Use in Page Objects:
```
export class HomePage {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly navigation: NavigationComponent;
  
  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.navigation = new NavigationComponent(page);
  }

  async goto() {
    await this.page.goto('/');
  }
}

// In test:
test('navigate to products', async ({ page }) => {
  const homePage = new HomePage(page);
  await homePage.goto();
  await homePage.navigation.goToProducts();
});
```

Base Page Pattern:
------------------

BasePage.ts:
```
export class BasePage {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly navigation: NavigationComponent;
  readonly footer: FooterComponent;

  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.navigation = new NavigationComponent(page);
    this.footer = new FooterComponent(page);
  }

  async waitForPageLoad() {
    await this.page.waitForLoadState('networkidle');
  }

  async takeScreenshot(name: string) {
    await this.page.screenshot({ path: `screenshots/${name}.png` });
  }
}
```

Extend in specific pages:
```
export class ProductPage extends BasePage {
  readonly addToCartButton: Locator;

  constructor(page: Page) {
    super(page);  // Initialize base page
    this.addToCartButton = page.locator('#add-to-cart');
  }

  async goto(productId: string) {
    await this.page.goto(`/products/${productId}`);
    await this.waitForPageLoad();  // From BasePage
  }
}
```

Page Factory Pattern (Using Fixtures):
---------------------------------------

pages/index.ts:
```
import { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { LoginPage } from './LoginPage';
import { DashboardPage } from './DashboardPage';
import { ProductPage } from './ProductPage';

export class PageFactory {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly loginPage: LoginPage;
  readonly dashboardPage: DashboardPage;
  readonly productPage: ProductPage;

  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
    this.dashboardPage = new DashboardPage(page);
    this.productPage = new ProductPage(page);
  }
}
```

Custom fixture:
```
import { test as base } from '@playwright/test';
import { PageFactory } from './pages';

type MyFixtures = {
  pages: PageFactory;
};

export const test = base.extend<MyFixtures>({
  pages: async ({ page }, use) => {
    await use(new PageFactory(page));
  }
});

export { expect } from '@playwright/test';
```

Usage in tests:
```
import { test, expect } from './fixtures';

test('login flow', async ({ pages }) => {
  await pages.loginPage.goto();
  await pages.loginPage.login('user', 'pass');
  await pages.dashboardPage.expectWelcomeMessage('user');
});
```

Data-Driven Page Objects:
--------------------------

```
export class FormPage {
  readonly page: Page;

  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
  }

  async fillForm(data: Record<string, string>) {
    for (const [field, value] of Object.entries(data)) {
      await this.page.fill(`#${field}`, value);
    }
  }

  async submit() {
    await this.page.click('#submit');
  }
}

// Usage:
test('fill contact form', async ({ page }) => {
  const formPage = new FormPage(page);
  
  await formPage.fillForm({
    name: 'John Doe',
    email: 'john@example.com',
    phone: '555-1234',
    message: 'Hello!'
  });
  
  await formPage.submit();
});
```

Best Practices:
---------------

1. ✅ One Page Object Per Page/Component:
   ```
   LoginPage.ts
   DashboardPage.ts
   ProductListPage.ts
   ProductDetailPage.ts
   ```

2. ✅ Use Descriptive Method Names:
   ```
   // ✓ Clear
   async loginWithCredentials(username: string, password: string)
   
   // ✗ Vague
   async login()
   ```

3. ✅ Return Page Objects for Chaining:
   ```
   async login(user: string, pass: string): Promise<DashboardPage> {
     await this.usernameInput.fill(user);
     await this.passwordInput.fill(pass);
     await this.loginButton.click();
     return new DashboardPage(this.page);
   }
   ```

4. ✅ Keep Tests Simple:
   ```
   // ✓ Readable
   test('add to cart', async ({ pages }) => {
     await pages.productPage.goto('123');
     await pages.productPage.addToCart();
     await pages.cartPage.expectItemCount(1);
   });
   
   // ✗ Too much logic in test
   test('add to cart', async ({ page }) => {
     await page.goto('/products/123');
     await page.locator('#size').selectOption('L');
     await page.locator('#quantity').fill('1');
     await page.locator('#add-to-cart').click();
     // ... etc
   });
   ```

5. ✅ Don't Assert in Page Objects (Usually):
   ```
   // ✓ Separate assertion
   async getWelcomeText(): Promise<string> {
     return await this.welcomeMessage.textContent() || '';
   }
   
   // In test:
   const text = await dashboardPage.getWelcomeText();
   expect(text).toBe('Welcome!');
   
   // ⚠️ Exception: Common assertions can be in page object
   async expectWelcomeMessage(username: string) {
     await expect(this.welcomeMessage).toHaveText(`Welcome, ${username}!`);
   }
   ```

6. ✅ Use TypeScript for Type Safety:
   ```
   export interface UserCredentials {
     username: string;
     password: string;
   }

   async login(credentials: UserCredentials) {
     await this.usernameInput.fill(credentials.username);
     await this.passwordInput.fill(credentials.password);
     await this.loginButton.click();
   }
   ```

Real-World Complete Example:
-----------------------------

E-commerce Test Suite Structure:
```
tests/
├── pages/
│   ├── BasePage.ts
│   ├── LoginPage.ts
│   ├── ProductListPage.ts
│   ├── ProductDetailPage.ts
│   ├── CartPage.ts
│   ├── CheckoutPage.ts
│   └── index.ts (PageFactory)
├── components/
│   ├── NavigationComponent.ts
│   ├── FooterComponent.ts
│   └── SearchComponent.ts
├── fixtures/
│   └── pageFixtures.ts
└── specs/
    ├── login.spec.ts
    ├── shopping.spec.ts
    └── checkout.spec.ts
```

shopping.spec.ts:
```
import { test, expect } from '../fixtures/pageFixtures';

test.describe('Shopping Flow', () => {
  test('add product to cart', async ({ pages }) => {
    // Login
    await pages.loginPage.goto();
    await pages.loginPage.login('testuser', 'password');
    
    // Browse products
    await pages.productListPage.goto();
    await pages.productListPage.filterByCategory('Electronics');
    
    // Select product
    await pages.productListPage.clickProduct('Laptop Pro 15');
    
    // Add to cart
    await pages.productDetailPage.selectOption('RAM', '16GB');
    await pages.productDetailPage.selectOption('Color', 'Silver');
    await pages.productDetailPage.addToCart();
    
    // Verify cart
    await pages.cartPage.goto();
    await pages.cartPage.expectItemCount(1);
    await pages.cartPage.expectTotal('$1299.99');
  });
});
```

================================================================================

14. AUTHENTICATION STATE MANAGEMENT
====================================

The Challenge:
--------------

Problem: Logging in for every test is slow

Traditional approach:
```
test('view profile', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.fill('#username', 'user');
  await page.fill('#password', 'pass');
  await page.click('#login');  // 2-3 seconds
  
  await page.goto('/profile');  // Actual test
  // ...
});

test('edit settings', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.fill('#username', 'user');
  await page.fill('#password', 'pass');
  await page.click('#login');  // Another 2-3 seconds!
  
  await page.goto('/settings');  // Actual test
  // ...
});
```

Result: Slow test suite (100 tests × 3 seconds = 5 minutes just for login!)

Playwright Solution: Storage State
-----------------------------------

Concept:
1. Login once
2. Save authentication state (cookies, localStorage)
3. Reuse state for all tests
4. Each test starts already logged in!

Implementation:
---------------

Step 1: Create Global Setup

global-setup.ts:
```
import { chromium, FullConfig } from '@playwright/test';

async function globalSetup(config: FullConfig) {
  const browser = await chromium.launch();
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  const page = await context.newPage();
  
  // Login
  await page.goto('https://example.com/login');
  await page.fill('#username', process.env.USER_NAME || 'testuser');
  await page.fill('#password', process.env.PASSWORD || 'password');
  await page.click('#login');
  
  // Wait for login to complete
  await page.waitForURL('**/dashboard');
  
  // Save authentication state
  await context.storageState({ path: 'auth.json' });
  
  await browser.close();
}

export default globalSetup;
```

Step 2: Configure Playwright

playwright.config.ts:
```
import { defineConfig } from '@playwright/test';

export default defineConfig({
  globalSetup: require.resolve('./global-setup'),
  
  use: {
    // All tests use this storage state
    storageState: 'auth.json',
  },
});
```

Step 3: Write Tests

```
test('view profile', async ({ page }) => {
  // Already logged in!
  await page.goto('/profile');
  
  await expect(page.locator('.username')).toHaveText('testuser');
});

test('edit settings', async ({ page }) => {
  // Already logged in!
  await page.goto('/settings');
  
  await page.fill('#email', 'newemail@example.com');
  await page.click('#save');
});
```

Result: Tests run instantly without repeated login!

auth.json Structure:
--------------------

```json
{
  "cookies": [
    {
      "name": "session_id",
      "value": "abc123xyz",
      "domain": "example.com",
      "path": "/",
      "expires": 1735689600,
      "httpOnly": true,
      "secure": true,
      "sameSite": "Lax"
    }
  ],
  "origins": [
    {
      "origin": "https://example.com",
      "localStorage": [
        {
          "name": "user_token",
          "value": "eyJhbGc..."
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

Multiple User Roles:
--------------------

Scenario: Different user types (admin, regular user, guest)

Setup for Multiple States:

auth-setup.ts:
```
import { chromium } from '@playwright/test';

export async function setupAuth() {
  const browser = await chromium.launch();
  
  // Admin user
  const adminContext = await browser.newContext();
  const adminPage = await adminContext.newPage();
  await adminPage.goto('https://example.com/login');
  await adminPage.fill('#username', 'admin');
  await adminPage.fill('#password', 'adminpass');
  await adminPage.click('#login');
  await adminPage.waitForURL('**/admin');
  await adminContext.storageState({ path: 'auth-admin.json' });
  
  // Regular user
  const userContext = await browser.newContext();
  const userPage = await userContext.newPage();
  await userPage.goto('https://example.com/login');
  await userPage.fill('#username', 'user');
  await userPage.fill('#password', 'userpass');
  await userPage.click('#login');
  await userPage.waitForURL('**/dashboard');
  await userContext.storageState({ path: 'auth-user.json' });
  
  await browser.close();
}
```

Use in Tests:

```
// Admin tests
test.use({ storageState: 'auth-admin.json' });

test('admin dashboard', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/admin');
  await expect(page.locator('h1')).toHaveText('Admin Panel');
});

// User tests
test.use({ storageState: 'auth-user.json' });

test('user dashboard', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/dashboard');
  await expect(page.locator('h1')).toHaveText('Dashboard');
});
```

Project-Based Configuration:
-----------------------------

playwright.config.ts:
```
export default defineConfig({
  globalSetup: require.resolve('./auth-setup'),
  
  projects: [
    {
      name: 'logged-out',
      use: {
        // No storage state - tests public pages
      },
    },
    {
      name: 'user',
      use: {
        storageState: 'auth-user.json',
      },
    },
    {
      name: 'admin',
      use: {
        storageState: 'auth-admin.json',
      },
    },
  ],
});
```

Run specific project:
```
npx playwright test --project=admin
npx playwright test --project=user
npx playwright test --project=logged-out
```

Organize tests:
```
tests/
├── admin/
│   ├── users.spec.ts
│   └── settings.spec.ts
├── user/
│   ├── profile.spec.ts
│   └── orders.spec.ts
└── public/
    ├── homepage.spec.ts
    └── login.spec.ts
```

Setup Project Pattern (Best Practice):
---------------------------------------

playwright.config.ts:
```
export default defineConfig({
  projects: [
    // Setup project - runs first
    {
      name: 'setup',
      testMatch: /.*\.setup\.ts/,
    },
    
    // Tests that depend on setup
    {
      name: 'logged-in-tests',
      use: {
        storageState: 'playwright/.auth/user.json',
      },
      dependencies: ['setup'],
    },
  ],
});
```

auth.setup.ts:
```
import { test as setup } from '@playwright/test';

setup('authenticate', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('https://example.com/login');
  await page.fill('#username', 'user');
  await page.fill('#password', 'pass');
  await page.click('#login');
  
  await page.waitForURL('**/dashboard');
  
  // Save storage state
  await page.context().storageState({ 
    path: 'playwright/.auth/user.json' 
  });
});
```

Regular test:
```
// This runs AFTER auth.setup.ts
test('dashboard test', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/dashboard');
  // Already logged in!
});
```

Conditional Authentication:
---------------------------

Some tests need auth, some don't:

```
test.describe('Public pages', () => {
  test.use({ storageState: { cookies: [], origins: [] } }); // No auth
  
  test('homepage', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    // Not logged in
  });
});

test.describe('Protected pages', () => {
  test.use({ storageState: 'auth.json' }); // With auth
  
  test('profile', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/profile');
    // Logged in
  });
});
```

API-Based Authentication:
--------------------------

If your app uses JWT tokens or API login:

```
import { test as setup } from '@playwright/test';

setup('authenticate via API', async ({ request }) => {
  // Login via API
  const response = await request.post('https://api.example.com/login', {
    data: {
      username: 'testuser',
      password: 'password'
    }
  });
  
  const { token } = await response.json();
  
  // Create storage state with token
  const storageState = {
    cookies: [],
    origins: [
      {
        origin: 'https://example.com',
        localStorage: [
          {
            name: 'auth_token',
            value: token
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  };
  
  // Save to file
  const fs = require('fs');
  fs.writeFileSync('auth.json', JSON.stringify(storageState));
});
```

Session Expiration Handling:
-----------------------------

Handle cases where session expires:

```
import { test as base } from '@playwright/test';

export const test = base.extend({
  page: async ({ page }, use) => {
    // Check if logged in before each test
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    if (page.url().includes('/login')) {
      // Session expired, login again
      await page.fill('#username', 'user');
      await page.fill('#password', 'pass');
      await page.click('#login');
      await page.waitForURL('**/dashboard');
    }
    
    await use(page);
  }
});
```

Best Practices:
---------------

1. ✅ Use Global Setup for Single User:
   ```
   globalSetup: require.resolve('./global-setup')
   ```

2. ✅ Use Setup Project for Multiple States:
   ```
   projects: [
     { name: 'setup', testMatch: /.*\.setup\.ts/ },
     { name: 'tests', dependencies: ['setup'] }
   ]
   ```

3. ✅ Store Auth Files in .gitignore:
   ```
   playwright/.auth/
   auth*.json
   ```

4. ✅ Use Environment Variables for Credentials:
   ```
   process.env.TEST_USER
   process.env.TEST_PASSWORD
   ```

5. ✅ Verify Auth State Before Tests:
   ```
   test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
     await page.goto('/dashboard');
     await expect(page).toHaveURL('/dashboard'); // Not /login
   });
   ```

6. ❌ Don't Commit Auth Files:
   ```
   # .gitignore
   auth.json
   auth-*.json
   playwright/.auth/
   ```

Real-World Performance Impact:
-------------------------------

Without Storage State:
```
Login time: 3 seconds per test
100 tests × 3 seconds = 5 minutes just for login
Total suite time: 15 minutes
```

With Storage State:
```
Global setup: 3 seconds (one time)
Login time per test: 0 seconds
Total suite time: 10 minutes
```

Savings: 33% faster test suite!

================================================================================

15. TEST ORGANIZATION AND HOOKS
================================

Test Structure:
---------------

Basic test file:
```
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test('single test', async ({ page }) => {
  // Test code
});
```

Grouped tests:
```
test.describe('Login Feature', () => {
  test('successful login', async ({ page }) => {
    // Test 1
  });
  
  test('failed login', async ({ page }) => {
    // Test 2
  });
});
```

Nested groups:
```
test.describe('E-commerce', () => {
  
  test.describe('Shopping Cart', () => {
    test('add item', async ({ page }) => {});
    test('remove item', async ({ page }) => {});
  });
  
  test.describe('Checkout', () => {
    test('payment', async ({ page }) => {});
    test('confirmation', async ({ page }) => {});
  });
});
```

Test Hooks:
-----------

1. beforeAll - Runs once before all tests in a describe block:
```
test.describe('Dashboard Tests', () => {
  
  test.beforeAll(async ({ browser }) => {
    // Setup that runs once
    console.log('Setting up test data...');
    // Create test data in database
  });
  
  test('test 1', async ({ page }) => {});
  test('test 2', async ({ page }) => {});
  
  test.afterAll(async () => {
    // Cleanup that runs once
    console.log('Cleaning up test data...');
  });
});
```

2. beforeEach - Runs before each test:
```
test.describe('Product Tests', () => {
  
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    // Runs before EACH test
    await page.goto('/products');
  });
  
  test('filter products', async ({ page }) => {
    // Page already on /products
    await page.click('[data-filter="electronics"]');
  });
  
  test('sort products', async ({ page }) => {
    // Page already on /products
    await page.selectOption('#sort', 'price-asc');
  });
});
```

3. afterEach - Runs after each test:
```
test.describe('Form Tests', () => {
  
  test.afterEach(async ({ page }) => {
    // Cleanup after each test
    await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.clear());
  });
  
  test('submit form', async ({ page }) => {
    // Test code
  });
  
  test('validate form', async ({ page }) => {
    // Test code
  });
});
```

4. afterAll - Runs once after all tests:
```
test.describe('API Tests', () => {
  
  test.afterAll(async () => {
    // Cleanup after all tests
    console.log('Closing database connection...');
  });
  
  test('create user', async ({ request }) => {});
  test('update user', async ({ request }) => {});
});
```

Hook Execution Order:
---------------------

```
test.describe('Suite', () => {
  test.beforeAll(() => console.log('1. beforeAll'));
  test.beforeEach(() => console.log('2. beforeEach'));
  test.afterEach(() => console.log('4. afterEach'));
  test.afterAll(() => console.log('5. afterAll'));
  
  test('test 1', () => console.log('3. test 1'));
  test('test 2', () => console.log('3. test 2'));
});

// Output:
// 1. beforeAll
// 2. beforeEach
// 3. test 1
// 4. afterEach
// 2. beforeEach
// 3. test 2
// 4. afterEach
// 5. afterAll
```

Nested Hooks:
-------------

```
test.describe('Outer', () => {
  test.beforeEach(() => console.log('Outer beforeEach'));
  
  test.describe('Inner', () => {
    test.beforeEach(() => console.log('Inner beforeEach'));
    
    test('nested test', () => console.log('Test'));
  });
});

// Output:
// Outer beforeEach
// Inner beforeEach
// Test
```

Real-World Examples:
--------------------

Example 1: Authentication Setup
```
test.describe('Authenticated Tests', () => {
  
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    // Login before each test
    await page.goto('/login');
    await page.fill('#username', 'testuser');
    await page.fill('#password', 'password');
    await page.click('#login');
    await page.waitForURL('/dashboard');
  });
  
  test('view profile', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/profile');
    // Already logged in
  });
  
  test('edit settings', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/settings');
    // Already logged in
  });
});
```

Example 2: Test Data Management
```
test.describe('User Management', () => {
  let userId: string;
  
  test.beforeAll(async ({ request }) => {
    // Create test user once
    const response = await request.post('/api/users', {
      data: { name: 'Test User', email: 'test@example.com' }
    });
    const user = await response.json();
    userId = user.id;
  });
  
  test('view user', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto(`/users/${userId}`);
    await expect(page.locator('.name')).toHaveText('Test User');
  });
  
  test('edit user', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto(`/users/${userId}/edit`);
    await page.fill('#name', 'Updated Name');
    await page.click('#save');
  });
  
  test.afterAll(async ({ request }) => {
    // Delete test user
    await request.delete(`/api/users/${userId}`);
  });
});
```

Example 3: Database State
```
test.describe('Shopping Cart', () => {
  
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    // Clear cart before each test
    await page.goto('/cart');
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      localStorage.removeItem('cart');
      sessionStorage.clear();
    });
    await page.reload();
  });
  
  test('add item to cart', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/products/123');
    await page.click('#add-to-cart');
    await expect(page.locator('.cart-count')).toHaveText('1');
  });
  
  test('remove item from cart', async ({ page }) => {
    // Cart is empty due to beforeEach
    await page.goto('/cart');
    await expect(page.locator('.empty-cart')).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```

Test Annotations and Metadata:
-------------------------------

1. test.skip():
```
test.skip('not ready yet', async ({ page }) => {
  // This test won't run
});

// Conditional skip
test('browser-specific', async ({ page, browserName }) => {
  test.skip(browserName === 'webkit', 'Safari not supported');
  // Test code
});
```

2. test.only():
```
test.only('debug this test', async ({ page }) => {
  // Only this test runs
});

test('other test', async ({ page }) => {
  // This won't run
});
```

3. test.fixme():
```
test.fixme('known bug #123', async ({ page }) => {
  // Marked as broken, will be skipped
});
```

4. test.slow():
```
test('slow operation', async ({ page }) => {
  test.slow(); // 3x timeout
  
  // Long-running operation
  await page.goto('/generate-report');
  await page.waitForSelector('.report-complete', { timeout: 60000 });
});
```

5. test.fail():
```
test('expected to fail', async ({ page }) => {
  test.fail(); // Test passing = failure, test failing = pass
  
  // Known browser bug
  await page.click('#broken-button');
});
```

Test Info Object:
-----------------

Access test metadata:

```
test('example', async ({ page }, testInfo) => {
  console.log(testInfo.title);        // Test title
  console.log(testInfo.file);         // File path
  console.log(testInfo.line);         // Line number
  console.log(testInfo.project.name); // Project name
  console.log(testInfo.retry);        // Retry count
  
  // Conditional logic
  if (testInfo.project.name === 'mobile') {
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 375, height: 667 });
  }
  
  // Screenshots on failure
  testInfo.attach('screenshot', {
    body: await page.screenshot(),
    contentType: 'image/png'
  });
});
```

Test Timeout:
-------------

```
// Global timeout (config)
export default defineConfig({
  timeout: 30000, // 30 seconds per test
});

// Per test
test('custom timeout', async ({ page }) => {
  test.setTimeout(60000); // 60 seconds for this test
  
  // Long operation
  await page.goto('/slow-page');
});

// Per describe block
test.describe('Slow Tests', () => {
  test.setTimeout(120000); // 2 minutes for all tests in this block
  
  test('test 1', async ({ page }) => {});
  test('test 2', async ({ page }) => {});
});
```

Test Retries:
-------------

```
// Global retries (config)
export default defineConfig({
  retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0,
});

// Per test
test('flaky test', async ({ page }) => {
  test.info().retries = 3; // Retry up to 3 times
  
  // Potentially flaky operation
});
```

Test Tags (Playwright 1.42+):
------------------------------

```
test('login @smoke @critical', async ({ page }) => {
  // Tagged test
});

test('admin panel @admin @slow', async ({ page }) => {
  // Tagged test
});

// Run tagged tests:
// npx playwright test --grep @smoke
// npx playwright test --grep "@smoke|@critical"
// npx playwright test --grep-invert @slow
```

Parallel Execution Control:
----------------------------

```
// Run tests in series (one at a time)
test.describe.serial('Dependent Tests', () => {
  test('step 1', async ({ page }) => {});
  test('step 2', async ({ page }) => {});
  test('step 3', async ({ page }) => {});
});

// Run all tests in parallel (default)
test.describe.parallel('Independent Tests', () => {
  test('test 1', async ({ page }) => {});
  test('test 2', async ({ page }) => {});
});
```

Test Steps:
-----------

Break down test into logical steps:

```
test('checkout flow', async ({ page }) => {
  await test.step('Add product to cart', async () => {
    await page.goto('/products/123');
    await page.click('#add-to-cart');
  });
  
  await test.step('Proceed to checkout', async () => {
    await page.goto('/cart');
    await page.click('#checkout');
  });
  
  await test.step('Fill shipping information', async () => {
    await page.fill('#address', '123 Main St');
    await page.fill('#city', 'Boston');
    await page.fill('#zip', '02101');
  });
  
  await test.step('Complete payment', async () => {
    await page.fill('#card-number', '4111111111111111');
    await page.fill('#expiry', '12/25');
    await page.click('#submit-payment');
  });
});
```

Benefits:
- Better test reports (shows which step failed)
- Easier debugging
- Clear test structure

Best Practices:
---------------

1. ✅ Use beforeEach for Common Setup:
   ```
   test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
     await page.goto('/products');
   });
   ```

2. ✅ Use afterEach for Cleanup:
   ```
   test.afterEach(async ({ page }) => {
     await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.clear());
   });
   ```

3. ✅ Group Related Tests:
   ```
   test.describe('Shopping Cart', () => {
     // All cart tests together
   });
   ```

4. ✅ Use Descriptive Names:
   ```
   test.describe('User Authentication', () => {
     test('should login with valid credentials', async ({ page }) => {});
     test('should show error with invalid credentials', async ({ page }) => {});
   });
   ```

5. ❌ Don't Overuse test.only:
   ```
   // Remove before committing!
   test.only('debug test', async ({ page }) => {});
   ```

6. ✅ Use test.slow() for Genuinely Slow Tests:
   ```
   test('generate report', async ({ page }) => {
     test.slow(); // Don't abuse - only for truly slow operations
   });
   ```

7. ✅ Organize by Feature:
   ```
   tests/
   ├── auth/
   │   ├── login.spec.ts
   │   └── signup.spec.ts
   ├── products/
   │   ├── list.spec.ts
   │   └── detail.spec.ts
   └── cart/
       └── checkout.spec.ts
   ```

PART III: ADVANCED TOPICS
