PART V: PRACTICAL EXAMPLES AND BEST PRACTICES
================================================================================

27. E-COMMERCE APPLICATION TESTING
===================================

Complete E-commerce Test Suite
-------------------------------

Page Objects:
```typescript
// pages/ProductPage.ts
import { Page, Locator } from '@playwright/test';

export class ProductPage {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly productTitle: Locator;
  readonly productPrice: Locator;
  readonly addToCartButton: Locator;
  readonly quantityInput: Locator;
  readonly sizeSelector: Locator;
  readonly colorSelector: Locator;
  readonly productImage: Locator;
  readonly reviewsSection: Locator;
  
  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.productTitle = page.locator('h1.product-title');
    this.productPrice = page.locator('.product-price');
    this.addToCartButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add to Cart' });
    this.quantityInput = page.locator('#quantity');
    this.sizeSelector = page.locator('#size-select');
    this.colorSelector = page.locator('#color-select');
    this.productImage = page.locator('.product-image img');
    this.reviewsSection = page.locator('#reviews');
  }
  
  async goto(productId: string) {
    await this.page.goto(`/products/${productId}`);
  }
  
  async addToCart(quantity: number = 1, size?: string, color?: string) {
    if (size) {
      await this.sizeSelector.selectOption(size);
    }
    if (color) {
      await this.colorSelector.selectOption(color);
    }
    
    await this.quantityInput.fill(quantity.toString());
    await this.addToCartButton.click();
    
    // Wait for cart update
    await this.page.waitForResponse('**/api/cart/add');
  }
  
  async getPrice(): Promise<number> {
    const priceText = await this.productPrice.textContent();
    return parseFloat(priceText!.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''));
  }
  
  async expectProductDetails(expected: {
    title?: string;
    price?: number;
    inStock?: boolean;
  }) {
    if (expected.title) {
      await expect(this.productTitle).toHaveText(expected.title);
    }
    if (expected.price) {
      const actualPrice = await this.getPrice();
      expect(actualPrice).toBe(expected.price);
    }
    if (expected.inStock !== undefined) {
      const stockStatus = this.page.locator('.stock-status');
      if (expected.inStock) {
        await expect(stockStatus).toHaveText(/In Stock/i);
      } else {
        await expect(stockStatus).toHaveText(/Out of Stock/i);
      }
    }
  }
}

// pages/CartPage.ts
export class CartPage {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly cartItems: Locator;
  readonly cartTotal: Locator;
  readonly checkoutButton: Locator;
  readonly emptyCartMessage: Locator;
  
  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.cartItems = page.locator('.cart-item');
    this.cartTotal = page.locator('.cart-total');
    this.checkoutButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Checkout' });
    this.emptyCartMessage = page.locator('.empty-cart');
  }
  
  async goto() {
    await this.page.goto('/cart');
  }
  
  async getItemCount(): Promise<number> {
    return await this.cartItems.count();
  }
  
  async getTotal(): Promise<number> {
    const totalText = await this.cartTotal.textContent();
    return parseFloat(totalText!.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''));
  }
  
  async removeItem(productName: string) {
    const item = this.page.locator('.cart-item', { hasText: productName });
    await item.locator('.remove-button').click();
    
    // Wait for cart update
    await this.page.waitForResponse('**/api/cart/remove');
  }
  
  async updateQuantity(productName: string, quantity: number) {
    const item = this.page.locator('.cart-item', { hasText: productName });
    await item.locator('.quantity-input').fill(quantity.toString());
    await item.locator('.update-button').click();
    
    await this.page.waitForResponse('**/api/cart/update');
  }
  
  async proceedToCheckout() {
    await this.checkoutButton.click();
    await this.page.waitForURL('**/checkout');
  }
}

// pages/CheckoutPage.ts
export class CheckoutPage {
  readonly page: Page;
  readonly emailInput: Locator;
  readonly shippingForm: Locator;
  readonly paymentForm: Locator;
  readonly placeOrderButton: Locator;
  
  constructor(page: Page) {
    this.page = page;
    this.emailInput = page.getByLabel('Email');
    this.shippingForm = page.locator('#shipping-form');
    this.paymentForm = page.locator('#payment-form');
    this.placeOrderButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Place Order' });
  }
  
  async fillShippingInfo(info: {
    firstName: string;
    lastName: string;
    address: string;
    city: string;
    state: string;
    zip: string;
    phone: string;
  }) {
    await this.shippingForm.getByLabel('First Name').fill(info.firstName);
    await this.shippingForm.getByLabel('Last Name').fill(info.lastName);
    await this.shippingForm.getByLabel('Address').fill(info.address);
    await this.shippingForm.getByLabel('City').fill(info.city);
    await this.shippingForm.getByLabel('State').selectOption(info.state);
    await this.shippingForm.getByLabel('ZIP Code').fill(info.zip);
    await this.shippingForm.getByLabel('Phone').fill(info.phone);
  }
  
  async fillPaymentInfo(info: {
    cardNumber: string;
    cardName: string;
    expiry: string;
    cvv: string;
  }) {
    await this.paymentForm.getByLabel('Card Number').fill(info.cardNumber);
    await this.paymentForm.getByLabel('Name on Card').fill(info.cardName);
    await this.paymentForm.getByLabel('Expiry Date').fill(info.expiry);
    await this.paymentForm.getByLabel('CVV').fill(info.cvv);
  }
  
  async placeOrder() {
    await this.placeOrderButton.click();
    await this.page.waitForURL('**/order/confirmation/**');
  }
}
```

```typescript
// tests/ecommerce.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { ProductPage } from '../pages/ProductPage';
import { CartPage } from '../pages/CartPage';
import { CheckoutPage } from '../pages/CheckoutPage';

test.describe('E-commerce Shopping Flow', () => {
  test('complete purchase journey', async ({ page }) => {
    const productPage = new ProductPage(page);
    const cartPage = new CartPage(page);
    const checkoutPage = new CheckoutPage(page);
    
    // Step 1: View product
    await test.step('View product details', async () => {
      await productPage.goto('laptop-pro-15');
      await productPage.expectProductDetails({
        title: 'Laptop Pro 15"',
        price: 1299.99,
        inStock: true
      });
    });
    
    // Step 2: Add to cart
    await test.step('Add product to cart', async () => {
      await productPage.addToCart(1, 'Silver', '16GB RAM');
      
      await expect(page.locator('.cart-notification')).toHaveText('Added to cart');
      await expect(page.locator('.cart-count')).toHaveText('1');
    });
    
    // Step 3: View cart
    await test.step('Review cart', async () => {
      await cartPage.goto();
      
      expect(await cartPage.getItemCount()).toBe(1);
      expect(await cartPage.getTotal()).toBe(1299.99);
    });
    
    // Step 4: Update quantity
    await test.step('Update quantity', async () => {
      await cartPage.updateQuantity('Laptop Pro 15"', 2);
      
      expect(await cartPage.getTotal()).toBe(2599.98);
    });
    
    // Step 5: Proceed to checkout
    await test.step('Proceed to checkout', async () => {
      await cartPage.proceedToCheckout();
    });
    
    // Step 6: Fill shipping info
    await test.step('Fill shipping information', async () => {
      await checkoutPage.fillShippingInfo({
        firstName: 'John',
        lastName: 'Doe',
        address: '123 Main St',
        city: 'Boston',
        state: 'MA',
        zip: '02101',
        phone: '555-0123'
      });
    });
    
    // Step 7: Fill payment info
    await test.step('Fill payment information', async () => {
      await checkoutPage.fillPaymentInfo({
        cardNumber: '4242424242424242',
        cardName: 'John Doe',
        expiry: '12/25',
        cvv: '123'
      });
    });
    
    // Step 8: Place order
    await test.step('Place order', async () => {
      await checkoutPage.placeOrder();
      
      // Verify confirmation page
      await expect(page.locator('.order-confirmation')).toBeVisible();
      await expect(page.locator('.order-number')).toBeVisible();
      await expect(page.locator('.order-total')).toHaveText('$2,599.98');
    });
  });
  
  test('apply discount code', async ({ page }) => {
    const productPage = new ProductPage(page);
    const cartPage = new CartPage(page);
    
    await productPage.goto('laptop-pro-15');
    await productPage.addToCart();
    
    await cartPage.goto();
    
    // Apply discount code
    await page.fill('#discount-code', 'SAVE20');
    await page.click('#apply-discount');
    
    await expect(page.locator('.discount-applied')).toHaveText('20% off');
    
    const originalPrice = 1299.99;
    const discountedPrice = originalPrice * 0.8;
    expect(await cartPage.getTotal()).toBe(discountedPrice);
  });
  
  test('remove item from cart', async ({ page }) => {
    const productPage = new ProductPage(page);
    const cartPage = new CartPage(page);
    
    // Add items
    await productPage.goto('laptop-pro-15');
    await productPage.addToCart();
    
    await productPage.goto('wireless-mouse');
    await productPage.addToCart();
    
    await cartPage.goto();
    expect(await cartPage.getItemCount()).toBe(2);
    
    // Remove one item
    await cartPage.removeItem('Wireless Mouse');
    
    expect(await cartPage.getItemCount()).toBe(1);
    await expect(page.locator('.cart-item', { hasText: 'Laptop Pro' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator('.cart-item', { hasText: 'Wireless Mouse' })).toBeHidden();
  });
  
  test('handle out of stock items', async ({ page }) => {
    const productPage = new ProductPage(page);
    
    await productPage.goto('out-of-stock-item');
    
    await expect(productPage.addToCartButton).toBeDisabled();
    await expect(page.locator('.stock-status')).toHaveText('Out of Stock');
    await expect(page.locator('.notify-button')).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```

```typescript
// tests/forms.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Form Testing Best Practices', () => {
  
  test('registration form with all field types', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/register');
    
    // Text inputs
    await page.getByLabel('Username').fill('testuser123');
    await page.getByLabel('Email').fill('test@example.com');
    
    // Password
    await page.getByLabel('Password').fill('SecurePass123!');
    await page.getByLabel('Confirm Password').fill('SecurePass123!');
    
    // Select dropdown
    await page.getByLabel('Country').selectOption('United States');
    
    // Radio buttons
    await page.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Male' }).check();
    
    // Checkboxes
    await page.getByLabel('Subscribe to newsletter').check();
    await page.getByLabel('I agree to terms').check();
    
    // Date picker
    await page.getByLabel('Date of Birth').fill('1990-01-15');
    
    // File upload
    await page.getByLabel('Profile Picture').setInputFiles('profile.jpg');
    
    // Submit
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Register' }).click();
    
    // Verify success
    await expect(page).toHaveURL('/welcome');
    await expect(page.locator('.success-message')).toHaveText('Registration successful!');
  });
  
  test('validation - empty fields', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/register');
    
    // Submit without filling
    await page.click('#submit');
    
    // Check all required field errors
    await expect.soft(page.locator('#username-error')).toHaveText('Username is required');
    await expect.soft(page.locator('#email-error')).toHaveText('Email is required');
    await expect.soft(page.locator('#password-error')).toHaveText('Password is required');
  });
  
  test('validation - invalid formats', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/register');
    
    // Invalid email
    await page.fill('#email', 'invalid-email');
    await page.click('#submit');
    await expect(page.locator('#email-error')).toHaveText('Invalid email format');
    
    // Weak password
    await page.fill('#password', '123');
    await page.click('#submit');
    await expect(page.locator('#password-error')).toContainText('at least 8 characters');
    
    // Password mismatch
    await page.fill('#password', 'SecurePass123!');
    await page.fill('#confirm-password', 'DifferentPass');
    await page.click('#submit');
    await expect(page.locator('#confirm-error')).toHaveText('Passwords do not match');
  });
  
  test('real-time validation', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/register');
    
    const emailInput = page.getByLabel('Email');
    const emailError = page.locator('#email-error');
    const emailSuccess = page.locator('#email-success');
    
    // Type invalid email
    await emailInput.fill('test');
    await expect(emailError).toBeVisible();
    await expect(emailSuccess).toBeHidden();
    
    // Type partial email
    await emailInput.fill('test@');
    await expect(emailError).toBeVisible();
    
    // Type valid email
    await emailInput.fill('test@example.com');
    await expect(emailError).toBeHidden();
    await expect(emailSuccess).toBeVisible();
  });
  
  test('autocomplete and suggestions', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/search-form');
    
    const searchInput = page.getByPlaceholder('Search...');
    
    // Type to trigger autocomplete
    await searchInput.fill('play');
    
    // Wait for suggestions
    await expect(page.locator('.autocomplete-suggestions')).toBeVisible();
    
    // Verify suggestions
    await expect(page.locator('.suggestion-item')).toHaveCount(5);
    await expect(page.locator('.suggestion-item').first()).toContainText('play');
    
    // Click suggestion
    await page.locator('.suggestion-item', { hasText: 'Playwright' }).click();
    
    await expect(searchInput).toHaveValue('Playwright');
    await expect(page.locator('.autocomplete-suggestions')).toBeHidden();
  });
  
  test('multi-step form', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/multi-step-form');
    
    // Step 1: Personal Info
    await test.step('Personal Information', async () => {
      await page.getByLabel('First Name').fill('John');
      await page.getByLabel('Last Name').fill('Doe');
      await page.getByLabel('Email').fill('john@example.com');
      
      await page.click('button:has-text("Next")');
      
      await expect(page.locator('.step-indicator')).toHaveText('2 of 3');
    });
    
    // Step 2: Address
    await test.step('Address Information', async () => {
      await page.getByLabel('Street Address').fill('123 Main St');
      await page.getByLabel('City').fill('Boston');
      await page.getByLabel('State').selectOption('MA');
      await page.getByLabel('ZIP Code').fill('02101');
      
      await page.click('button:has-text("Next")');
      
      await expect(page.locator('.step-indicator')).toHaveText('3 of 3');
    });
    
    // Step 3: Review and Submit
    await test.step('Review and Submit', async () => {
      // Verify all entered data
      await expect(page.locator('.review-name')).toHaveText('John Doe');
      await expect(page.locator('.review-email')).toHaveText('john@example.com');
      await expect(page.locator('.review-address')).toContainText('123 Main St');
      
      await page.click('button:has-text("Submit")');
      
      await expect(page.locator('.success-message')).toBeVisible();
    });
  });
  
  test('dynamic form fields', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/dynamic-form');
    
    // Initially one phone field
    await expect(page.locator('.phone-field')).toHaveCount(1);
    
    // Add more fields
    await page.click('#add-phone');
    await expect(page.locator('.phone-field')).toHaveCount(2);
    
    await page.click('#add-phone');
    await expect(page.locator('.phone-field')).toHaveCount(3);
    
    // Fill fields
    await page.locator('.phone-field').nth(0).fill('555-0001');
    await page.locator('.phone-field').nth(1).fill('555-0002');
    await page.locator('.phone-field').nth(2).fill('555-0003');
    
    // Remove middle field
    await page.locator('.remove-phone').nth(1).click();
    await expect(page.locator('.phone-field')).toHaveCount(2);
    
    // Verify remaining values
    await expect(page.locator('.phone-field').nth(0)).toHaveValue('555-0001');
    await expect(page.locator('.phone-field').nth(1)).toHaveValue('555-0003');
  });
  
  test('file upload with validation', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/upload-form');
    
    // Test file size validation
    await test.step('File size validation', async () => {
      await page.setInputFiles('#file', 'large-file.pdf');
      await expect(page.locator('.error')).toHaveText('File size exceeds 10MB limit');
    });
    
    // Test file type validation
    await test.step('File type validation', async () => {
      await page.setInputFiles('#file', 'document.txt');
      await expect(page.locator('.error')).toHaveText('Only PDF, JPG, PNG files allowed');
    });
    
    // Test successful upload
    await test.step('Successful upload', async () => {
      await page.setInputFiles('#file', 'valid-document.pdf');
      await expect(page.locator('.success')).toBeVisible();
      await expect(page.locator('.filename')).toHaveText('valid-document.pdf');
    });
    
    // Test multiple files
    await test.step('Multiple file upload', async () => {
      await page.setInputFiles('#multiple-files', [
        'file1.jpg',
        'file2.png',
        'file3.pdf'
      ]);
      
      await expect(page.locator('.uploaded-file')).toHaveCount(3);
    });
  });
});
```

29. API TESTING PATTERNS

Complete API Testing Examples
------------------------------

```typescript
// tests/api.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('API Testing', () => {
  
  let apiContext;
  let authToken;
  
  test.beforeAll(async ({ playwright }) => {
    // Create API context
    apiContext = await playwright.request.newContext({
      baseURL: 'https://api.example.com',
      extraHTTPHeaders: {
        'Accept': 'application/json',
        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
      }
    });
    
    // Authenticate
    const loginResponse = await apiContext.post('/auth/login', {
      data: {
        username: 'testuser',
        password: 'password123'
      }
    });
    
    const loginData = await loginResponse.json();
    authToken = loginData.token;
  });
  
  test.afterAll(async () => {
    await apiContext.dispose();
  });
  
  test('GET request - fetch user', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.get('/users/123', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      }
    });
    
    expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy();
    expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
    
    const user = await response.json();
    expect(user).toMatchObject({
      id: 123,
      username: expect.any(String),
      email: expect.stringContaining('@'),
      createdAt: expect.any(String)
    });
  });
  
  test('POST request - create user', async () => {
    const newUser = {
      username: `user_${Date.now()}`,
      email: `test${Date.now()}@example.com`,
      firstName: 'Test',
      lastName: 'User'
    };
    
    const response = await apiContext.post('/users', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      },
      data: newUser
    });
    
    expect(response.status()).toBe(201);
    
    const createdUser = await response.json();
    expect(createdUser).toMatchObject({
      ...newUser,
      id: expect.any(Number),
      createdAt: expect.any(String)
    });
  });
  
  test('PUT request - update user', async () => {
    const updateData = {
      firstName: 'Updated',
      lastName: 'Name',
      email: 'updated@example.com'
    };
    
    const response = await apiContext.put('/users/123', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      },
      data: updateData
    });
    
    expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy();
    
    const updatedUser = await response.json();
    expect(updatedUser.firstName).toBe('Updated');
    expect(updatedUser.lastName).toBe('Name');
  });
  
  test('PATCH request - partial update', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.patch('/users/123', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      },
      data: {
        email: 'newemail@example.com'
      }
    });
    
    expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy();
    const user = await response.json();
    expect(user.email).toBe('newemail@example.com');
  });
  
  test('DELETE request - remove user', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.delete('/users/999', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      }
    });
    
    expect(response.status()).toBe(204);
  });
  
  test('error handling - 404 Not Found', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.get('/users/99999', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      }
    });
    
    expect(response.status()).toBe(404);
    
    const error = await response.json();
    expect(error).toMatchObject({
      error: 'User not found',
      statusCode: 404
    });
  });
  
  test('error handling - 401 Unauthorized', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.get('/users/123', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': 'Bearer invalid-token'
      }
    });
    
    expect(response.status()).toBe(401);
  });
  
  test('error handling - 400 Bad Request', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.post('/users', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      },
      data: {
        // Missing required fields
        username: 'test'
      }
    });
    
    expect(response.status()).toBe(400);
    
    const error = await response.json();
    expect(error.message).toContain('email is required');
  });
  
  test('pagination', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.get('/users', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      },
      params: {
        page: 1,
        limit: 10
      }
    });
    
    expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy();
    
    const data = await response.json();
    expect(data).toMatchObject({
      items: expect.any(Array),
      total: expect.any(Number),
      page: 1,
      limit: 10,
      totalPages: expect.any(Number)
    });
    
    expect(data.items.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10);
  });
  
  test('filtering and sorting', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.get('/users', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      },
      params: {
        role: 'admin',
        sort: 'createdAt',
        order: 'desc'
      }
    });
    
    const data = await response.json();
    
    // Verify all users are admins
    data.items.forEach(user => {
      expect(user.role).toBe('admin');
    });
    
    // Verify sorted by createdAt descending
    for (let i = 0; i < data.items.length - 1; i++) {
      const current = new Date(data.items[i].createdAt);
      const next = new Date(data.items[i + 1].createdAt);
      expect(current.getTime()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(next.getTime());
    }
  });
  
  test('response headers', async () => {
    const response = await apiContext.get('/users/123', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      }
    });
    
    const headers = response.headers();
    expect(headers['content-type']).toContain('application/json');
    expect(headers['x-rate-limit-remaining']).toBeDefined();
  });
  
  test('response time performance', async () => {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    
    await apiContext.get('/users/123', {
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${authToken}`
      }
    });
    
    const endTime = Date.now();
    const responseTime = endTime - startTime;
    
    expect(responseTime).toBeLessThan(1000); // Should respond within 1 second
  });
});
```

```typescript
// auth.setup.ts - Global Setup
import { test as setup, expect } from '@playwright/test';

const authFile = 'playwright/.auth/user.json';

setup('authenticate', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/login');
  
  await page.getByLabel('Username').fill('testuser');
  await page.getByLabel('Password').fill('password123');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in' }).click();
  
  // Wait for redirect
  await page.waitForURL('/dashboard');
  
  // Verify logged in
  await expect(page.getByText('Welcome, testuser')).toBeVisible();
  
  // Save storage state
  await page.context().storageState({ path: authFile });
});

// playwright.config.ts
export default defineConfig({
  projects: [
    // Setup project
    { name: 'setup', testMatch: /.*\.setup\.ts/ },
    
    // Logged in tests
    {
      name: 'chromium',
      use: {
        ...devices['Desktop Chrome'],
        storageState: authFile,
      },
      dependencies: ['setup'],
    },
  ],
});

// tests/authenticated.spec.ts
test.describe('Authenticated Tests', () => {
  test('user dashboard', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    // Already logged in!
    await expect(page.locator('.user-menu')).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```

```typescript
setup('authenticate as admin', async ({ page }) => {

  await page.fill('#username', 'user');
  await page.fill('#password', 'user123');

    { name: 'setup-user', testMatch: /auth-user\.setup\.ts/ },
    
    {
      name: 'admin-tests',
      use: { storageState: 'playwright/.auth/admin.json' },
      dependencies: ['setup-admin'],
```

Token-based Authentication:
```typescript
// api-auth.setup.ts
import { test as setup } from '@playwright/test';

setup('get auth token', async ({ request }) => {
  const response = await request.post('https://api.example.com/auth/login', {
    data: {
      username: 'testuser',
      password: 'password123'
    }
  });
  
  const { token } = await response.json();
  
  // Save token to environment
  process.env.AUTH_TOKEN = token;
  
  // Or save to file
  const fs = require('fs');
  fs.writeFileSync('playwright/.auth/token.txt', token);
});

// tests/api-with-auth.spec.ts
test('API call with token', async ({ request }) => {
  const token = process.env.AUTH_TOKEN;
  
  const response = await request.get('/api/protected-data', {
    headers: {
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
    }
  });
  
  expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy();
});
```

Session Persistence:
```typescript
test('session persists across page reloads', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/dashboard');
  
  // Verify logged in
  await expect(page.locator('.username')).toHaveText('testuser');
  
  // Reload page
  await page.reload();
  
  // Still logged in
  await expect(page.locator('.username')).toHaveText('testuser');
});

test('logout clears session', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/dashboard');
  
  // Logout
  await page.click('#logout');
  
  // Redirected to login
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/login');
  
  // Try to access protected page
  await page.goto('/dashboard');
  
  // Redirected back to login
  await expect(page).toHaveURL('/login');
});
```

31. VISUAL REGRESSION TESTING PATTERNS
=======================================

Screenshot Comparison Strategies
---------------------------------

```typescript
// tests/visual-regression.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Visual Regression Testing', () => {
  
  test('full page screenshot', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('homepage.png', {
      fullPage: true
    });
  });
  
  test('component screenshot', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    
    // Screenshot specific elements
    await expect(page.locator('header')).toHaveScreenshot('header.png');
    await expect(page.locator('.hero-section')).toHaveScreenshot('hero.png');
    await expect(page.locator('footer')).toHaveScreenshot('footer.png');
  });
  
  test('mask dynamic content', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('dashboard.png', {
      mask: [
        page.locator('.timestamp'),      // Mask timestamps
        page.locator('.live-data'),      // Mask live data
        page.locator('.user-avatar'),    // Mask user-specific content
        page.locator('.random-ads')      // Mask ads
      ]
    });
  });
  
  test('different viewports', async ({ page }) => {
    // Desktop
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1920, height: 1080 });
    await page.goto('/');
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('homepage-desktop.png');
    
    // Tablet
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 768, height: 1024 });
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('homepage-tablet.png');
    
    // Mobile
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 375, height: 667 });
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('homepage-mobile.png');
  });
  
  test('wait for animations', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/animated-page');
    
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('page.png', {
      animations: 'disabled'  // Disable CSS animations
    });
  });
  
  test('with threshold', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('homepage.png', {
      maxDiffPixels: 100,        // Allow 100 pixels difference
      maxDiffPixelRatio: 0.1,    // Allow 10% difference
      threshold: 0.2             // Color threshold
    });
  });
  
  test('cross-browser comparison', async ({ page, browserName }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    
    // Different baseline for each browser
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot(`homepage-${browserName}.png`);
  });
  
  test('responsive design validation', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    
    const breakpoints = [
      { width: 320, height: 568, name: 'mobile-small' },
      { width: 375, height: 667, name: 'mobile-medium' },
      { width: 768, height: 1024, name: 'tablet' },
      { width: 1366, height: 768, name: 'laptop' },
      { width: 1920, height: 1080, name: 'desktop' }
    ];
    
    for (const bp of breakpoints) {
      await page.setViewportSize({ width: bp.width, height: bp.height });
      await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot(`homepage-${bp.name}.png`);
    }
  });
  
  test('dark mode comparison', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    
    // Light mode
    await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: 'light' });
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('homepage-light.png');
    
    // Dark mode
    await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: 'dark' });
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('homepage-dark.png');
  });
  
  test('scroll position snapshots', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/long-page');
    
    // Top of page
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('page-top.png');
    
    // Middle of page
    await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, document.body.scrollHeight / 2));
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('page-middle.png');
    
    // Bottom of page
    await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, document.body.scrollHeight));
    await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot('page-bottom.png');
  });
  
  test('hover states', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/buttons');
    
    const button = page.locator('#primary-button');
    
    // Normal state
    await expect(button).toHaveScreenshot('button-normal.png');
    
    // Hover state
    await button.hover();
    await expect(button).toHaveScreenshot('button-hover.png');
    
    // Focus state
    await button.focus();
    await expect(button).toHaveScreenshot('button-focus.png');
  });
  
  test('loading states', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    // Initial state
    await expect(page.locator('.data-section')).toHaveScreenshot('loading.png');
    
    // Loaded state
    await page.waitForSelector('.data-loaded');
    await expect(page.locator('.data-section')).toHaveScreenshot('loaded.png');
  });
});

// Update all snapshots when needed:
// npx playwright test --update-snapshots
```

32. NETWORK MOCKING AND INTERCEPTION
=====================================

API Mocking Patterns
--------------------

```typescript
// tests/network-mocking.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Network Mocking', () => {
  
  test('mock API response', async ({ page }) => {
    // Mock GET request
    await page.route('**/api/users', route => {
      route.fulfill({
        status: 200,
        contentType: 'application/json',
        body: JSON.stringify([
          { id: 1, name: 'User One' },
          { id: 2, name: 'User Two' },
          { id: 3, name: 'User Three' }
        ])
      });
    });
    
    await page.goto('/users');
    
    // Verify mocked data is displayed
    await expect(page.locator('.user-item')).toHaveCount(3);
    await expect(page.locator('.user-item').first()).toContainText('User One');
  });
  
  test('mock different HTTP methods', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.route('**/api/users', route => {
      const method = route.request().method();
      
      if (method === 'GET') {
        route.fulfill({
          status: 200,
          body: JSON.stringify([{ id: 1, name: 'Test User' }])
        });
      } else if (method === 'POST') {
        route.fulfill({
          status: 201,
          body: JSON.stringify({ id: 2, name: 'New User' })
        });
      } else if (method === 'DELETE') {
        route.fulfill({ status: 204 });
      }
    });
    
    await page.goto('/users');
    
    // Trigger POST
    await page.click('#add-user');
    await expect(page.locator('.success')).toHaveText('User created');
  });
  
  test('mock error responses', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.route('**/api/data', route => {
      route.fulfill({
        status: 500,
        contentType: 'application/json',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          error: 'Internal Server Error',
          message: 'Database connection failed'
        })
      });
    });
    
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    await expect(page.locator('.error-message')).toHaveText('Failed to load data');
  });
  
  test('mock slow network', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.route('**/api/products', async route => {
      // Simulate slow network
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 3000));
      
      route.fulfill({
        status: 200,
        body: JSON.stringify([{ id: 1, name: 'Product' }])
      });
    });
    
    await page.goto('/products');
    
    // Loading indicator should be visible
    await expect(page.locator('.loading')).toBeVisible();
    
    // Data should load after delay
    await expect(page.locator('.product-item')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 5000 });
  });
  
  test('conditional mocking', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.route('**/api/users/**', route => {
      const url = route.request().url();
      
      if (url.includes('/users/1')) {
        route.fulfill({
          status: 200,
          body: JSON.stringify({ id: 1, name: 'Admin', role: 'admin' })
        });
      } else if (url.includes('/users/2')) {
        route.fulfill({
          status: 404,
          body: JSON.stringify({ error: 'User not found' })
        });
      } else {
        route.continue();  // Use real API
      }
    });
    
    await page.goto('/users/1');
    await expect(page.locator('.user-role')).toHaveText('admin');
    
    await page.goto('/users/2');
    await expect(page.locator('.error')).toHaveText('User not found');
  });
  
  test('intercept and modify request', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.route('**/api/**', route => {
      const headers = route.request().headers();
      
      route.continue({
        headers: {
          ...headers,
          'Authorization': 'Bearer fake-token',
          'X-Custom-Header': 'test-value'
        }
      });
    });
    
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
  });
  
  test('block resources', async ({ page }) => {
    // Block images, fonts, and analytics
    await page.route('**/*.{png,jpg,jpeg,svg,gif}', route => route.abort());
    await page.route('**/*.{woff,woff2,ttf}', route => route.abort());
    await page.route('**/analytics/**', route => route.abort());
    await page.route('**/tracking/**', route => route.abort());
    
    await page.goto('/');
    
    // Page should load faster without these resources
  });
  
  test('mock from HAR file', async ({ page }) => {
    // Record HAR file first (one-time)
    // Then replay
    await page.routeFromHAR('recordings/api-responses.har', {
      url: '**/api/**',
      update: false  // Use recorded responses
    });
    
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    // Uses responses from HAR file
  });
  
  test('spy on network requests', async ({ page }) => {
    const requests: any[] = [];
    
    page.on('request', request => {
      requests.push({
        url: request.url(),
        method: request.method(),
        headers: request.headers()
      });
    });
    
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    await page.click('#load-data');
    
    // Verify API was called
    const apiRequests = requests.filter(r => r.url.includes('/api/'));
    expect(apiRequests.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    
    const dataRequest = requests.find(r => r.url.includes('/api/data'));
    expect(dataRequest).toBeDefined();
    expect(dataRequest.method).toBe('GET');
  });
  
  test('wait for specific request/response', async ({ page }) => {
    const responsePromise = page.waitForResponse(
      response => response.url().includes('/api/users') && response.status() === 200
    );
    
    await page.goto('/users');
    await page.click('#refresh');
    
    const response = await responsePromise;
    const data = await response.json();
    
    expect(data).toBeInstanceOf(Array);
    expect(data.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
  
  test('mock GraphQL queries', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.route('**/graphql', route => {
      const postData = route.request().postDataJSON();
      
      if (postData.query.includes('getUser')) {
        route.fulfill({
          status: 200,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            data: {
              user: {
                id: '123',
                name: 'Test User',
                email: 'test@example.com'
              }
            }
          })
        });
      } else if (postData.query.includes('getPosts')) {
        route.fulfill({
          status: 200,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            data: {
              posts: [
                { id: '1', title: 'Post 1' },
                { id: '2', title: 'Post 2' }
              ]
            }
          })
        });
      }
    });
    
    await page.goto('/profile');
  });
});
```

33. PERFORMANCE TESTING
========================

Performance Monitoring Patterns
--------------------------------

```typescript
// tests/performance.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Performance Testing', () => {
  
  test('measure page load time', async ({ page }) => {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    
    await page.goto('https://example.com');
    
    const endTime = Date.now();
    const loadTime = endTime - startTime;
    
    console.log(`Page load time: ${loadTime}ms`);
    expect(loadTime).toBeLessThan(3000); // Should load within 3 seconds
  });
  
  test('web vitals - Core Web Vitals', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('https://example.com');
    
    const webVitals = await page.evaluate(() => {
      return new Promise<any>(resolve => {
        let vitals: any = {};
        
        // LCP - Largest Contentful Paint
        new PerformanceObserver((list) => {
          const entries = list.getEntries();
          const lastEntry = entries[entries.length - 1];
          vitals.lcp = lastEntry.renderTime || lastEntry.loadTime;
        }).observe({ entryTypes: ['largest-contentful-paint'] });
        
        // FID - First Input Delay
        new PerformanceObserver((list) => {
          const firstInput = list.getEntries()[0];
          vitals.fid = (firstInput as any).processingStart - firstInput.startTime;
        }).observe({ entryTypes: ['first-input'] });
        
        // CLS - Cumulative Layout Shift
        let clsValue = 0;
        new PerformanceObserver((list) => {
          for (const entry of list.getEntries()) {
            if (!(entry as any).hadRecentInput) {
              clsValue += (entry as any).value;
            }
          }
          vitals.cls = clsValue;
        }).observe({ entryTypes: ['layout-shift'] });
        
        setTimeout(() => resolve(vitals), 5000);
      });
    });
    
    console.log('Web Vitals:', webVitals);
    
    // Google's recommended thresholds
    expect(webVitals.lcp).toBeLessThan(2500);  // LCP < 2.5s
    expect(webVitals.cls).toBeLessThan(0.1);   // CLS < 0.1
  });
  
  test('navigation timing', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('https://example.com');
    
    const timing = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const perf = performance.getEntriesByType('navigation')[0] as PerformanceNavigationTiming;
      
      return {
        dns: perf.domainLookupEnd - perf.domainLookupStart,
        tcp: perf.connectEnd - perf.connectStart,
        request: perf.responseStart - perf.requestStart,
        response: perf.responseEnd - perf.responseStart,
        dom: perf.domContentLoadedEventEnd - perf.domContentLoadedEventStart,
        load: perf.loadEventEnd - perf.loadEventStart,
        total: perf.loadEventEnd - perf.fetchStart
      };
    });
    
    console.log('Navigation Timing:', timing);
    
    expect(timing.dns).toBeLessThan(100);     // DNS < 100ms
    expect(timing.tcp).toBeLessThan(100);     // TCP < 100ms
    expect(timing.request).toBeLessThan(200); // Request < 200ms
    expect(timing.total).toBeLessThan(3000);  // Total < 3s
  });
  
  test('resource loading', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('https://example.com');
    
    const resources = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const entries = performance.getEntriesByType('resource');
      
      return entries.map(entry => ({
        name: entry.name,
        duration: entry.duration,
        size: (entry as PerformanceResourceTiming).transferSize
      }));
    });
    
    console.log(`Total resources: ${resources.length}`);
    
    // Check for slow resources
    const slowResources = resources.filter(r => r.duration > 1000);
    console.log(`Slow resources (>1s): ${slowResources.length}`);
    
    slowResources.forEach(r => {
      console.log(`  ${r.name}: ${r.duration.toFixed(2)}ms`);
    });
  });
  
  test('API response time', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    const startTime = Date.now();
    
    const response = await page.waitForResponse(
      response => response.url().includes('/api/data') && response.status() === 200
    );
    
    const endTime = Date.now();
    const responseTime = endTime - startTime;
    
    console.log(`API response time: ${responseTime}ms`);
    expect(responseTime).toBeLessThan(500); // API should respond within 500ms
  });
  
  test('lighthouse metrics', async ({ page }) => {
    // Note: Requires playwright-lighthouse or similar
    await page.goto('https://example.com');
    
    // Example using Navigation Timing API as approximation
    const metrics = await page.evaluate(() => ({
      fcp: performance.getEntriesByType('paint')
        .find(e => e.name === 'first-contentful-paint')?.startTime,
      tti: performance.timing.domInteractive - performance.timing.navigationStart,
      speedIndex: performance.timing.domContentLoadedEventEnd - performance.timing.navigationStart
    }));
    
    console.log('Performance Metrics:', metrics);
    
    expect(metrics.fcp).toBeLessThan(1800);  // FCP < 1.8s
  });
  
  test('bundle size analysis', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('https://example.com');
    
    const jsSize = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const scripts = performance.getEntriesByType('resource')
        .filter(r => r.name.endsWith('.js'));
      
      return scripts.reduce((total, script) => {
        return total + ((script as PerformanceResourceTiming).transferSize || 0);
      }, 0);
    });
    
    console.log(`Total JS size: ${(jsSize / 1024).toFixed(2)} KB`);
    expect(jsSize).toBeLessThan(500 * 1024); // JS bundle < 500KB
  });
  
  test('memory usage', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('https://example.com');
    
    const memory = await page.evaluate(() => {
      return (performance as any).memory ? {
        usedJSHeapSize: (performance as any).memory.usedJSHeapSize,
        totalJSHeapSize: (performance as any).memory.totalJSHeapSize,
        jsHeapSizeLimit: (performance as any).memory.jsHeapSizeLimit
      } : null;
    });
    
    if (memory) {
      console.log('Memory Usage:', {
        used: `${(memory.usedJSHeapSize / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`,
        total: `${(memory.totalJSHeapSize / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`,
        limit: `${(memory.jsHeapSizeLimit / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`
      });
    }
  });
});
```

34. BEST PRACTICES AND PATTERNS
================================

Playwright Testing Best Practices
----------------------------------

1. **Use Semantic Locators**
```typescript
// ❌ Bad: Fragile selectors
await page.click('div > button:nth-child(3)');
await page.click('.MuiButton-root-123');

// ✓ Good: Semantic, accessible selectors
await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' }).click();
await page.getByLabel('Email').fill('test@example.com');
await page.getByText('Welcome back').click();
```

2. **Avoid Hard Waits**
```typescript
// ❌ Bad: Arbitrary waits
await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
await page.click('#button');

// ✓ Good: Wait for specific conditions
await page.waitForSelector('#button', { state: 'visible' });
await page.click('#button');

// ✓ Better: Use auto-waiting
await page.click('#button');  // Auto-waits!
await expect(page.locator('.result')).toBeVisible();
```

3. **Use Page Object Model**
```typescript
// ❌ Bad: Direct page interactions in tests
test('login', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.fill('#username', 'user');
  await page.fill('#password', 'pass');
  await page.click('#login-btn');
});

// ✓ Good: Page Object Model
class LoginPage {
  constructor(private page: Page) {}
  
  async login(username: string, password: string) {
    await this.page.goto('/login');
    await this.page.fill('#username', username);
    await this.page.fill('#password', password);
    await this.page.click('#login-btn');
  }
}

test('login', async ({ page }) => {
  const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
  await loginPage.login('user', 'pass');
});
```

4. **Proper Test Organization**
```typescript
// ✓ Good: Organized with describe blocks and hooks
test.describe('User Management', () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/admin');
  });
  
  test.describe('Create User', () => {
    test('with valid data', async ({ page }) => {});
    test('with invalid email', async ({ page }) => {});
  });
  
  test.describe('Delete User', () => {
    test('with confirmation', async ({ page }) => {});
    test('cancel deletion', async ({ page }) => {});
  });
});
```

5. **Use Test Steps**
```typescript
// ✓ Good: Clear test steps
test('checkout flow', async ({ page }) => {
  await test.step('Add items to cart', async () => {
    await page.goto('/products/1');
    await page.click('#add-to-cart');
  });
  
  await test.step('Proceed to checkout', async () => {
    await page.goto('/cart');
    await page.click('#checkout');
  });
  
  await test.step('Complete payment', async () => {
    await page.fill('#card', '4242424242424242');
    await page.click('#submit');
  });
});
```

6. **Proper Error Handling**
```typescript
// ✓ Good: Descriptive assertions
await expect(page.locator('.success-message')).toHaveText(
  'User created successfully',
  { timeout: 5000 }
);

// ✓ Good: Soft assertions for multiple checks
await expect.soft(page.locator('.name')).toBeVisible();
await expect.soft(page.locator('.email')).toBeVisible();
await expect.soft(page.locator('.role')).toBeVisible();
```

7. **Reusable Fixtures**
```typescript
// ✓ Good: Custom fixtures
type MyFixtures = {
  authenticatedPage: Page;
};

export const test = base.extend<MyFixtures>({
  authenticatedPage: async ({ page }, use) => {
    await page.goto('/login');
    await page.fill('#username', 'testuser');
    await page.fill('#password', 'password');
    await page.click('#login');
    await use(page);
  }
});

test('dashboard', async ({ authenticatedPage }) => {
  await authenticatedPage.goto('/dashboard');
});
```

8. **Data-Driven Testing**
```typescript
// ✓ Good: Parameterized tests
const testCases = [
  { input: 'test@example.com', expected: true },
  { input: 'invalid-email', expected: false },
  { input: 'test@', expected: false }
];

for (const tc of testCases) {
  test(`validate email: ${tc.input}`, async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/signup');
    await page.fill('#email', tc.input);
    await page.click('#submit');
    
    if (tc.expected) {
      await expect(page.locator('.success')).toBeVisible();
    } else {
      await expect(page.locator('.error')).toBeVisible();
    }
  });
}
```

9. **Proper Clean-up**
```typescript
// ✓ Good: Clean up after tests
test.afterEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    localStorage.clear();
    sessionStorage.clear();
  });
});

test.afterAll(async ({ request }) => {
  // Delete test data
  await request.delete('/api/test-users');
});
```

10. **Parallel Execution**
```typescript
// playwright.config.ts
export default defineConfig({
  fullyParallel: true,
  workers: process.env.CI ? 2 : undefined,
  
  // For dependent tests
  projects: [
    {
      name: 'setup',
      testMatch: /.*\.setup\.ts/
    },
    {
      name: 'tests',
      dependencies: ['setup']
    }
  ]
});
```

This completes sections **31-34** (Visual Regression, Network Mocking, Performance, Best Practices).
Next batch will add sections **35-37** (final sections) and the Appendix to complete Part V.

35. CI/CD INTEGRATION EXAMPLES
===============================

Complete CI/CD Setup
--------------------

**GitHub Actions - Complete Workflow:**
```yaml
# .github/workflows/playwright.yml
name: Playwright Tests

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main, develop ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 2 * * *'  # Daily at 2 AM

jobs:
  test:
    name: Test on ${{ matrix.os }}
    timeout-minutes: 60
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest, windows-latest, macos-latest]
        node-version: [18, 20]
        browser: [chromium, firefox, webkit]
    
    steps:
    - name: Checkout code
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
    
    - name: Setup Node.js ${{ matrix.node-version }}
      uses: actions/setup-node@v4
      with:
        node-version: ${{ matrix.node-version }}
        cache: 'npm'
    
    - name: Install dependencies
      run: npm ci
    
    - name: Install Playwright Browsers
      run: npx playwright install --with-deps ${{ matrix.browser }}
    
    - name: Run Playwright tests
      run: npx playwright test --project=${{ matrix.browser }}
      env:
        BASE_URL: ${{ secrets.BASE_URL }}
        API_KEY: ${{ secrets.API_KEY }}
    
    - name: Upload test results
      if: always()
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
      with:
        name: playwright-results-${{ matrix.os }}-${{ matrix.browser }}
        path: test-results/
        retention-days: 30
    
    - name: Upload HTML report
      if: always()
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
      with:
        name: playwright-report-${{ matrix.os }}-${{ matrix.browser }}
        path: playwright-report/
        retention-days: 30
    
    - name: Upload trace
      if: failure()
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
      with:
        name: playwright-trace-${{ matrix.os }}-${{ matrix.browser }}
        path: test-results/**/trace.zip
        retention-days: 30
  
  report:
    name: Publish Report
    needs: test
    if: always()
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    
    steps:
    - name: Download artifacts
      uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
    
    - name: Publish to GitHub Pages
      uses: peaceiris/actions-gh-pages@v3
      with:
        github_token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
        publish_dir: ./playwright-report
```

**GitLab CI:**
```yaml
# .gitlab-ci.yml
stages:
  - test
  - report

variables:

test:chromium:
```

36. DEBUGGING TECHNIQUES
=========================

Advanced Debugging Strategies
------------------------------

```typescript
// tests/debugging-examples.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Debugging Techniques', () => {
  
  test('using pause for interactive debugging', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('https://example.com');
    
    // Pause execution - opens Playwright Inspector
    await page.pause();
    
    await page.click('#button');
  });
  
  test('step-by-step debugging with UI Mode', async ({ page }) => {
    // Run: npx playwright test --ui
    // Features:
    // - Time travel debugging
    // - Watch mode
    // - Pick locator
    // - Visual timeline
    
    await page.goto('/form');
    await page.fill('#username', 'testuser');
    await page.fill('#password', 'password');
    await page.click('#submit');
  });
  
  test('console logging and screenshots', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    // Log current URL
    console.log('Current URL:', page.url());
    
    // Log element count
    const itemCount = await page.locator('.item').count();
    console.log('Items found:', itemCount);
    
    // Take screenshot at specific point
    await page.screenshot({ path: 'debug-screenshot.png' });
    
    // Element screenshot
    await page.locator('.problematic-section').screenshot({
      path: 'element-debug.png'
    });
  });
  
  test('trace viewer for post-mortem debugging', async ({ page }) => {
    // Traces are enabled in playwright.config.ts:
    // use: { trace: 'on-first-retry' }
    
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.click('#button');
    
    // If test fails, trace is saved
    // View with: npx playwright show-trace trace.zip
    
    // Trace includes:
    // - Screenshots at every action
    // - DOM snapshots
    // - Network activity
    // - Console logs
    // - Source code
  });
  
  test('browser console monitoring', async ({ page }) => {
    const logs: string[] = [];
    const errors: string[] = [];
    
    page.on('console', msg => {
      console.log(`Browser ${msg.type()}: ${msg.text()}`);
      logs.push(msg.text());
    });
    
    page.on('pageerror', error => {
      console.log(`Page error: ${error.message}`);
      errors.push(error.message);
    });
    
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.click('#button');
    
    // Verify no errors
    expect(errors.length).toBe(0);
  });
  
  test('network activity debugging', async ({ page }) => {
    const requests: any[] = [];
    const responses: any[] = [];
    
    page.on('request', request => {
      requests.push({
        url: request.url(),
        method: request.method(),
        headers: request.headers()
      });
    });
    
    page.on('response', response => {
      responses.push({
        url: response.url(),
        status: response.status(),
        headers: response.headers()
      });
    });
    
    await page.goto('/dashboard');
    
    console.log(`Requests: ${requests.length}`);
    console.log(`Responses: ${responses.length}`);
    
    // Check for failed requests
    const failedResponses = responses.filter(r => r.status >= 400);
    console.log('Failed requests:', failedResponses);
  });
  
  test('slow motion for visual debugging', async ({ page }) => {
    // Run with: npx playwright test --headed --slow-mo=1000
    // Or configure in context
    
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.click('#button');
    
    // Each action delayed by 1000ms for visual inspection
  });
  
  test('codegen for generating selectors', async ({ page }) => {
    // Run: npx playwright codegen https://example.com
    // Features:
    // - Record actions
    // - Generate test code
    // - Pick locators
    // - Copy selector
    
    await page.goto('/');
    // Use codegen to find best selector
    await page.click('button[data-testid="submit"]');
  });
  
  test('debugging with test info', async ({ page }, testInfo) => {
    console.log('Test name:', testInfo.title);
    console.log('Project:', testInfo.project.name);
    console.log('Retry:', testInfo.retry);
    console.log('Duration:', testInfo.duration);
    
    if (testInfo.retry > 0) {
      console.log('This is a retry - adding extra logging');
      page.on('console', msg => console.log('Browser:', msg.text()));
    }
    
    await page.goto('/');
    
    // Save artifacts with test info
    const screenshotPath = testInfo.outputPath('screenshot.png');
    await page.screenshot({ path: screenshotPath });
  });
  
  test('conditional breakpoints', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/products');
    
    const items = await page.locator('.product-item').all();
    
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
      const price = await items[i].locator('.price').textContent();
      
      // Pause only if price is unexpected
      if (parseFloat(price!) > 1000) {
        console.log(`High price found: ${price}`);
        await page.pause();
      }
    }
  });
});
```

37. COMMON PITFALLS AND SOLUTIONS
==================================

Common Mistakes and How to Avoid Them
--------------------------------------

**1. Stale Element References**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Element reference can become stale
const button = await page.$('#button');
await page.reload();
await button?.click();  // Error: stale element

// ✓ Solution: Use locators (auto-refreshing)
const button = page.locator('#button');
await page.reload();
await button.click();  // Works! Fresh lookup
```

**2. Race Conditions**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Clicking before element is ready
await page.goto('/');
await page.locator('#load-data').click();
await page.locator('.result').textContent();  // May fail

// ✓ Solution: Wait for specific state
await page.goto('/');
await page.locator('#load-data').click();
await page.waitForSelector('.loading');
await page.waitForSelector('.loading', { state: 'hidden' });
await expect(page.locator('.result')).toBeVisible();
```

**3. Incorrect Selector Scope**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Selector too broad
await page.click('button');  // Which button?

// ✓ Solution: Be specific
await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' }).click();
await page.locator('#form').getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' }).click();
```

**4. Not Waiting for Navigation**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Not waiting for page load
await page.click('a[href="/next"]');
await page.locator('h1').textContent();  // May fail

// ✓ Solution: Wait for navigation
await Promise.all([
  page.waitForNavigation(),
  page.click('a[href="/next"]')
]);

// ✓ Better: Use goto or waitForURL
await page.click('a[href="/next"]');
await page.waitForURL('**/next');
```

**5. Hard-coded Timeouts**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Arbitrary waits
await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
await page.click('#button');

// ✓ Solution: Wait for specific conditions
await page.waitForSelector('#button', { state: 'visible' });
await page.click('#button');

// ✓ Better: Use auto-waiting
await page.click('#button');  // Auto-waits!
```

**6. Not Handling Dynamic Content**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Assuming content loads instantly
await page.goto('/dashboard');
const text = await page.locator('.stats').textContent();

// ✓ Solution: Wait for content to load
await page.goto('/dashboard');
await page.waitForResponse('**/api/stats');
await expect(page.locator('.stats')).not.toBeEmpty();
const text = await page.locator('.stats').textContent();
```

**7. Fragile Selectors**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Brittle selectors
await page.click('div > div > button:nth-child(3)');
await page.click('.css-abc123-def456');

// ✓ Solution: Semantic selectors
await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' }).click();
await page.getByTestId('submit-button').click();
```

**8. Not Cleaning Up Test Data**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Tests pollute each other
test('create user', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/admin');
  await page.fill('#username', 'testuser');
  await page.click('#create');
  // User left in database!
});

// ✓ Solution: Clean up after test
test('create user', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/admin');
  await page.fill('#username', 'testuser');
  await page.click('#create');
  
  // Cleanup
  test.afterEach(async ({ request }) => {
    await request.delete('/api/users/testuser');
  });
});
```

**9. Parallel Test Conflicts**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Tests sharing state
test('test 1', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.setItem('key', 'value1'));
});

test('test 2', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  const value = await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem('key'));
  // May get 'value1' from test 1!
});

// ✓ Solution: Use isolated contexts
test.use({ storageState: { cookies: [], origins: [] } });

test('test 1', async ({ page }) => {
  // Clean state
});

test('test 2', async ({ page }) => {
  // Clean state
});
```

**10. Incorrect Assertion Usage**
```typescript
// ❌ Problem: Non-retrying assertions
const text = await page.locator('.result').textContent();
expect(text).toBe('Success');  // No retry!

// ✓ Solution: Use Playwright's expect (auto-retries)
await expect(page.locator('.result')).toHaveText('Success');
```

================================================================================
APPENDIX: QUICK REFERENCE
================================================================================

A. ESSENTIAL COMMANDS
=====================

**Installation:**
```bash
npm init playwright@latest              # Initialize Playwright
npm install --save-dev @playwright/test # Manual install
npx playwright install                  # Install browsers
npx playwright install chromium         # Install specific browser
```

**Running Tests:**
```bash
npx playwright test                     # Run all tests
npx playwright test mytest.spec.ts      # Run specific file
npx playwright test --headed            # Run in headed mode
npx playwright test --ui                # Run in UI mode
npx playwright test --debug             # Run in debug mode
npx playwright test --project=chromium  # Run specific browser
npx playwright test -g "login"          # Run tests matching pattern
npx playwright test --last-failed       # Run only failed tests
```

**Debugging:**
```bash
npx playwright test --debug             # Debug mode
npx playwright test --ui                # UI mode (best for debugging)
npx playwright codegen                  # Generate test code
npx playwright codegen https://example.com  # Record from URL
npx playwright show-trace trace.zip     # View trace file
npx playwright show-report              # View HTML report
```

**Other Commands:**
```bash
npx playwright test --update-snapshots  # Update visual snapshots
npx playwright test --trace on          # Enable tracing
npx playwright test --workers=1         # Run sequentially
npx playwright test --shard=1/3         # Sharding (CI)
```

B. COMMON LOCATORS
==================

**By Role (Recommended):**
```typescript
page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit' })
page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Email' })
page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })
page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Home' })
page.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Agree' })
```

**By Text:**
```typescript
page.getByText('Click me')
page.getByText(/click me/i)  // Regex
page.getByText('Click', { exact: true })
```

**By Label:**
```typescript
page.getByLabel('Email address')
page.getByLabel('Password')
```

**By Placeholder:**
```typescript
page.getByPlaceholder('Enter your email')
```

**By Test ID:**
```typescript
page.getByTestId('submit-button')
```

**By CSS:**
```typescript
page.locator('#id')
page.locator('.class')
page.locator('[data-attr="value"]')
page.locator('div > button')
```

C. COMMON ASSERTIONS
====================

**Page Assertions:**
```typescript
await expect(page).toHaveURL('/dashboard')
await expect(page).toHaveTitle('Home Page')
```

**Element Assertions:**
```typescript
await expect(locator).toBeVisible()
await expect(locator).toBeHidden()
await expect(locator).toBeEnabled()
await expect(locator).toBeDisabled()
await expect(locator).toBeChecked()
await expect(locator).toBeFocused()
await expect(locator).toContainText('text')
await expect(locator).toHaveText('exact text')
await expect(locator).toHaveValue('value')
await expect(locator).toHaveAttribute('attr', 'value')
await expect(locator).toHaveCount(5)
await expect(locator).toHaveClass('class-name')
```

**API Assertions:**
```typescript
expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy()
expect(response.status()).toBe(200)
expect(await response.json()).toMatchObject({ id: 1 })
```

D. CONFIGURATION TEMPLATE
==========================

**Basic playwright.config.ts:**
```typescript
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './tests',
  fullyParallel: true,
  forbidOnly: !!process.env.CI,
  retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0,
  workers: process.env.CI ? 1 : undefined,
  reporter: 'html',
  
  use: {
    baseURL: 'http://localhost:3000',
    trace: 'on-first-retry',
    screenshot: 'only-on-failure',
    video: 'retain-on-failure',
  },
  
  projects: [
    {
      name: 'chromium',
      use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'] },
    },
    {
      name: 'firefox',
      use: { ...devices['Desktop Firefox'] },
    },
    {
      name: 'webkit',
      use: { ...devices['Desktop Safari'] },
    },
  ],
  
  webServer: {
    command: 'npm run start',
    url: 'http://localhost:3000',
    reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
  },
});
```

E. CHEAT SHEET
==============

**Test Structure:**
```typescript
test.describe('Group', () => {
  test.beforeAll(() => { /* runs once before all */ });
  test.beforeEach(() => { /* runs before each test */ });
  test.afterEach(() => { /* runs after each test */ });
  test.afterAll(() => { /* runs once after all */ });
  
  test('test name', async ({ page }) => {
    // Test code
  });
});
```

**Actions:**
```typescript
await page.goto(url)
await page.click(selector)
await page.fill(selector, text)
await page.selectOption(selector, value)
await page.check(selector)
await page.uncheck(selector)
await page.hover(selector)
await page.press(selector, key)
await page.type(selector, text)
await page.setInputFiles(selector, files)
```

**Waits:**
```typescript
await page.waitForSelector(selector)
await page.waitForURL(url)
await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle')
await page.waitForResponse(url)
await page.waitForRequest(url)
await page.waitForFunction(() => condition)
```

**Getting Values:**
```typescript
await page.textContent(selector)
await page.inputValue(selector)
await page.getAttribute(selector, name)
await page.title()
await page.url()
```
